### Day 46: work (Phase 3, week 7)

**What we learn today:**
- Topic words: *ett jobb / ett arbete* (a job, work), *en arbetsplats* (a workplace), *en chef* (a manager), *en kollega* (a colleague), *ett skift* (a shift), *ett möte* (a meeting), *en lön* (a salary), *en semester* (a holiday), *en arbetstid* (working hours), *en ansökan* (an application), *en intervju* (an interview), *ledig* (off work, free), *sjuk* (ill), *byta skift* (swap shifts). Sweden-Swedish says *sjukanmäla sig* for "call in sick"; this curriculum is not sure what Finland-Swedish workplaces say, so ask the teacher. Today's sentences avoid the word.
- **Cards today (load rule 1):** at most 8 new cards in total, error cards included. Card words: *en chef, en kollega, ett skift, byta skift, ledig, en semester, en arbetsplats, en lön*. Recognise only, no card: *en arbetstid, en ansökan, en intervju*, and *ett jobb / ett arbete, ett möte, sjuk*, which you met in earlier days. Each error card takes the place of the last card word.
- T8, T9 and T11 at work:
  1. *Mitt barn är sjukt, så jag måste stanna hemma i dag.* (My child is ill, so I have to stay at home today.)
  2. *Jag är tillbaka på onsdag.* (I'll be back on Wednesday.)
  3. *Kan du byta skift med mig på lördag?* (Can you swap shifts with me on Saturday?)
  4. *Jag kan ta ditt skift på söndag i stället.* (I can take your Sunday shift instead.)
  5. *Tyvärr kan jag inte jobba över i kväll, eftersom jag har en tid hos läkaren.* (Unfortunately I can't work late tonight, because I have a doctor's appointment.)
- Grammar: *ett barn* gives *sjukt* (G7); *så* keeps the normal order (G10); confirm with T1: *Okej, då säger vi så.* (OK, let's do that.)

**Why it matters for the exam:** "Work" is an official topic area [Official]. A message about an absence combines moves you already have: the apology (T9), the refusal (T8) and a suggestion (T11).

**How we learn it (60 min):**
- 0–8 Review: cards from Days 16, 32, 39, 43 and 45.
- 8–18 Topic words and sentences. Ladder on sentence 3: swap the day, add a reason with *eftersom*, then add sentence 4 as an offer.
- 18–33 Speaking: a) a voicemail, 20 s [Official sample timing]; b) a shift call, 4 turns × 20 s [Plan's practice limit]. Record both; redo the weaker one.
- 33–48 Writing (15 min).
- 48–56 Notice routine with a workplace notice: what, when, where, for whom, condition.
- 56–60 Error log; cards for the card words above (at most 8 new cards in total).

**Speaking output:**
- a) *Ditt barn är sjukt och du måste stanna hemma i dag. Lämna ett meddelande till din chef.* (Your child is ill and you must stay home today. Leave your manager a message.) Points (4): who; why; when you are back; what happens with your work.
- b) *Du har ett skift på lördag, men din syster gifter sig. Ring din kollega Mikko och fråga om ni kan byta skift.* (You work on Saturday, but your sister is getting married. Ask your colleague Mikko to swap.) The other voice: 1 *Hej, det är Mikko.* 2 *Vilket skift gäller det?* 3 *Jag jobbar redan på fredag. Kan du ta mitt skift på söndag?* 4 *Okej, då gör vi så.* Points (5): who and why you call; which shift; the reason; accept his offer; confirm and thank him.

**Writing output:** *Du ska gå på en kurs på torsdag och fredag nästa vecka. Skriv till din chef. Skriv när du är borta och varför, vem som kan ta hand om ditt arbete och hur man kan nå dig.* (You are on a course next Thursday and Friday. Tell your manager when and why you are away, who will cover your work, and how to reach you.) Required points (6): greeting; the days; the reason; the plan for your work; how to reach you; closing.

Model: *Hej Anna! Jag går på en kurs på torsdag och fredag nästa vecka, så jag är inte på jobbet de dagarna. Innan dess gör jag klart rapporten. Mikko kan svara på mina kunders frågor. Jag läser min e-post på kvällen, så du kan nå mig om det är bråttom. Hälsningar, Alex*

**Done when (Green / Amber / Red):** Green = the voicemail gives all 4 points, the call all 5 and the message all 6, on your own. Targets (not gates): 20 s for the voicemail; engine errors go on the error list (aim: 2 or fewer).

**Typical errors to watch:**
- *Mitt barn är sjuk.* → *Mitt barn är sjukt.* (G7)
- *Kan du att byta skift?* → *Kan du byta skift?* (G4)
- *I morgon jag är ledig.* → *I morgon är jag ledig.* (G1)

[Unreviewed Swedish: have a teacher check it]

**Book practice (*Förbered dig för allmän språkexamen*, pointers only; descriptions are our own words):** 2 task(s) today. Each replaces one of today's own prompts, or the redo of one; if nothing fits, it waits for a 90-minute day (see the note at the top of this file).
- `p79-D1`: p. 79-80, Dialog 1 (phone), T9: Tell your boss you are ill and cannot work; handover questions
- `p87-R10`: p. 87, Reagera 10, T2: Overworked: propose to your boss ways to reduce your workload

### Day 47: travel and transport (Phase 3, week 7)

**What we learn today:**
- Topic words: *ett tåg* (a train), *en buss* (a bus), *en spårvagn* (a tram), *en biljett* (a ticket), *en hållplats* (a stop), *en perrong* (a platform), *en resväska* (a suitcase), *ett bagage* (luggage), *en flygplats* (an airport), *ett flyg* (a flight), *en försening* (a delay), *en avgång / en ankomst* (a departure / an arrival), *försenad / inställd* (delayed / cancelled), *byta tåg* (change trains). The plan lists *inhiberad* as an unverified Finland-Swedish word for "cancelled": recognise it, and ask the teacher.
- **Cards today (load rule 1):** at most 8 new cards in total, error cards included. Card words: *en resväska, ett bagage, en flygplats, ett flyg, en biljett, en försening, en avgång / en ankomst, en hållplats*. Recognise only, no card: *en spårvagn, en perrong, byta tåg, inhiberad*, and *ett tåg, en buss, försenad / inställd*, which you met on Day 23. Each error card takes the place of the last card word.
- T12 and T2:
  1. *Min resväska kom inte med flyget från Frankfurt.* (My suitcase didn't come on the flight from Frankfurt.)
  2. *Den är stor och blå och har ett gult band på handtaget.* (It is big and blue and has a yellow strap on the handle.)
  3. *Kan ni skicka väskan hem till mig?* (Can you send the bag to my home?)
  4. *Tåget var två timmar försenat.* (The train was two hours late.)
  5. *Därför missade jag ett viktigt möte.* (So I missed an important meeting.)
- Grammar: *ett tåg* gives *försenat*, *ett band* gives *gult* (G7); *Därför* + verb (G1); a finished time takes the past: *kom inte, var, missade* (G6).

**Why it matters for the exam:** "Travel" is an official topic area [Official]. Delays and cancellations also carry the meaning-flip words *försenad, inställd* and *flyttad*, which change the meaning in listening and reading.

**How we learn it (60 min):**
- 0–8 Review: cards from Days 17, 33, 40, 44 and 46.
- 8–18 Topic words and sentences. Ladder on sentence 2: describe a black backpack, then a red suitcase.
- 18–33 Speaking: 6-turn call, 20 s per turn [Plan's practice limit]. Record, listen twice, redo once.
- 33–48 Writing (15 min).
- 48–56 Easy-news routine, shortened. Listen for any delay or cancellation.
- 56–60 Error log; cards for the card words above (at most 8 new cards in total).

**Speaking output:** card: *Du har kommit hem från en resa, men din resväska kom inte med flyget. Ring bagageservicen. Beskriv väskan och fråga när du kan få den.* (You are home from a trip, but your suitcase did not arrive. Call baggage services, describe it and ask when you will get it.) The other voice: 1 *Bagageservice, hej.* 2 *Vilket flyg kom du med?* 3 *Hur ser väskan ut?* 4 *Vad finns i väskan?* 5 *Vi tror att den är kvar i Frankfurt. Den kommer kanske i morgon.* 6 *Vart ska vi skicka den?* Points (7): who and why you call; which flight (from where, when); a description (2–3 details); what is in it; why it is urgent; your address; a close.

**Writing output:** *Ditt tåg från Helsingfors till Åbo var två timmar försenat, och du missade ett viktigt möte. Skriv till tågbolaget. Skriv vilket tåg det gällde, vad som hände, vilka problem det gav dig och vad du vill att bolaget gör. Ge dina kontaktuppgifter.* (Your train from Helsinki to Turku was two hours late and you missed an important meeting. Write to the rail company.) Required points (7): formal opening; which train (date, time, route); what happened; what it caused; what you want; contact details; formal closing. This curriculum does not know the real refund rules, so say only what you want.

Model: *Hej! Jag reste med tåget från Helsingfors till Åbo den 12 mars klockan 8.05. Tåget var två timmar försenat, och ingen informerade oss om varför. Därför missade jag ett viktigt möte. Biljetten kostade 25 euro, och jag skulle vilja få pengarna tillbaka. Ni når mig på 040 123 4567. Med vänliga hälsningar, Alex Kumar*

**Done when (Green / Amber / Red):** Green = the call and the complaint each give all 7 points, on your own. Targets (not gates): every turn inside 20 s; engine errors go on the error list (aim: 2 or fewer).

**Typical errors to watch:**
- *Tåget var två timmar försenad.* → *Tåget var två timmar försenat.* (G7)
- *Därför jag missade mötet.* → *Därför missade jag mötet.* (G1)
- *Min väska har inte kommit i går.* → *Min väska kom inte i går.* (G6)

[Unreviewed Swedish: have a teacher check it]

**Book practice (*Förbered dig för allmän språkexamen*, pointers only; descriptions are our own words):** 2 task(s) today. Each replaces one of today's own prompts, or the redo of one; if nothing fits, it waits for a 90-minute day (see the note at the top of this file).
- `p104-D5`: p. 104-105, Dialog 5, T4: At the station your train is missing; ask why, complain, ask for compensation.
- `p112-R8`: p. 112, Reagera 8, T2: Ask reception to book an airport taxi; give your flight details.

### Day 48: mixed prompts 1 (Phase 3, week 7)

**What we learn today:** no new topic list. Instead, words that appear on the plan's appendix prompt cards in sections A–F (request through apology), so a card never stops you. **Card words** (8, the load-rule maximum, error cards included): *förlänga* (extend), *ett lån* (a loan), *ett medlemskap* (a membership), *säga upp* (cancel a contract), *en påminnelse* (a reminder letter), *en avgift* (a fee), *en beställning* (an order), *en hantverkare* (a tradesperson). **Recognise only**, met on earlier days, no new card: *begagnad* (second-hand), *en kundtjänst* (customer service), *avboka* (cancel a booking), *tappa bort* (lose). And the adapt rule: 1 underline the card's points; 2 pick the template for the function; 3 fill every slot with the card's details; 4 delete any line the card does not ask for.

**Why it matters for the exam:** Every task and instruction is in Swedish only [Official], and the real prompts will be new to you. Today checks whether the templates transfer to cards you have not practised.

**How we learn it (60 min):**
- 0–8 Review: cards from Days 18, 34, 41, 45 and 47.
- 8–12 The 8 card words, English cue first; read the 4 recognise-only words once.
- 12–30 Three speaking prompts you have not practised (seen practice, below), back to back. About 15 s to read each card [Teacher, unofficial]; calls are 6 turns × 20 s [Plan's practice limit]. The helper plays the other voice, or you first write 6 questions for the other side and cover them.
- 30–51 The writing set: the three book Skriva tasks in today's Book practice, 7 minutes each [Plan's own, shorter than usual to build speed], about 40–60 words each. They are today's only writing; A6, D6 and F4 are not done today.
- 51–57 Notice routine, shortened.
- 57–60 Error log. Mark the three appendix speaking prompts as used, so they are not picked again.

**Speaking output (seen practice):**
- B2, buyer: *Du har sett en annons om en begagnad tv. Ring säljaren. Fråga om skick, ålder och pris, och försök få ett lägre pris.* (Call about a used TV; ask about condition, age and price; try to get it cheaper.)
- C3: *Internet hemma har inte fungerat på tre dagar. Ring företaget.* (Your home internet has not worked for three days.) Extra points: your customer number; what it stops you doing; ask about compensation.
- F5: *Du har lånat en bok av en vän och tappat bort den. Ring och berätta.* (You borrowed a friend's book and lost it.) Extra point: offer to buy a new one.

**Writing output:** today's single writing set is the three book Skriva tasks under Book practice (7 minutes each). The three appendix prompts below are **spares, not done today** (seen practice): use them on a 90-minute day or in the cycle after Day 84, each with its section's default points, and contact details in the formal texts.
- A6: *Skriv till en hyresvärd. Du har sett en annons om en lägenhet. Fråga hur stor den är, när man kan flytta in och om du kan se lägenheten.* (Ask a landlord about a flat in an ad: size, move-in date, a viewing.)
- D6: *Ditt gym har tagit betalt för en månad efter att du sa upp ditt medlemskap. Skriv och be om pengarna tillbaka.* (Your gym charged you after you cancelled.) Extra points: when you cancelled; the amount.
- F4: *Du glömde ett möte med din granne. Skriv och be om ursäkt.* (You forgot a meeting with your neighbour. Apologise.) Extra point: suggest a new time.

**Done when (Green / Amber / Red):** Green = the three calls and the three Skriva tasks each give all their points, the card's extra points included, on your own. Targets (not gates): every task in time (about 15 s to read a card, 20 s a turn, 7 minutes a text).

**Typical errors to watch:**
- A memorised line that answers nothing, such as *Det är bråttom* (It's urgent) when nothing is urgent. Delete it.
- Missing an extra point from the card, such as the amount in D6.
- In the buyer call, no offer. Use T3: *Kan du tänka dig att sälja den för [80] euro?* (Would you consider selling it for 80 euro?)

[Unreviewed Swedish: have a teacher check it]

**Book practice (*Förbered dig för allmän språkexamen*, pointers only; descriptions are our own words):** 3 task(s) today. The three Skriva tasks are today's three writing prompts and replace A6, D6 and F4 (a documented exception to the 2-task limit).
- `p75-S1`: p. 75, Skriva 1, T2: Forum post asking for advice: your health problem, symptoms, daily impact
- `p75-S2`: p. 75, Skriva 2, T10: Message to a colleague on long sick leave: ask, support, offer help
- `p90-S1`: p. 90, Skriva 1, T2: Desk note asking an absent colleague to swap a shift with you

### Day 49: light day (Phase 3, week 7)

**What we learn today:** nothing new. Due cards, and the Day 43 repair call re-recorded from memory with one detail changed (the light-day rule in 3.1). New card: *Din tvättmaskin läcker vatten, fast en reparatör var hos dig i måndags. Ring servicefirman. Förklara problemet, protestera och be om en ny tid.* (Your washing machine is leaking, although a repair person came on Monday. Call the service firm, explain, protest and ask for a new time.) Key lines: *Den läcker fortfarande.* (It is still leaking.) *Problemet är att den läcker när jag tvättar.* (The problem is that it leaks when I do the washing.) *Jag vill inte betala för ett nytt besök, eftersom det är samma fel.* (I don't want to pay for a new visit, because it is the same fault.)

**Why it matters for the exam:** The repair call is an official sample task type, 6 turns of 10–25 s [Official sample]. Repeating it from memory with a change keeps it ready without memorising one fixed answer.

**How we learn it (about 35 min, light day):**
- 0–15 All overdue cards.
- 15–25 The call, 6 turns × 20 s, recorded. Use the Day 43 voice lines: 1 *Servicefirman, hej.* 2 *Vad är problemet?* 3 *Vad gjorde reparatören?* 4 *Vi kan komma nästa vecka.* 5 *Torsdag går bra. Vad är adressen?* 6 *Bra, vi kommer på torsdag förmiddag.* Listen once for the 7 points.
- 25–35 Easy-news routine with one new clip.

**Speaking output:** the card above, 6 turns × 20 s [Plan's practice limit]. The 7 points from Day 43: who you are; what is broken; that it is still broken after a visit, and when the visit was; what the repair person did; a protest with a reason; a new time and your address; a close with the payment point.

**Writing output:** none (light day).

**Done when (Green / Amber / Red):** Green = the call gives all 7 points, with no notes, on your own, and the clip gives 3 facts and its numbers. Targets (not gates): no turn over 20 s; the overdue cards cleared.

**Typical errors to watch:**
- *Den fungerar inte fortfarande.* → *Den fungerar fortfarande inte.* (the normal order, as in 5.1; teacher to confirm)
- *En reparatör var här i måndag.* → *En reparatör var här i måndags.* (G9: *i måndags* is last Monday)

[Unreviewed Swedish: have a teacher check it]

### Day 50: culture and free time (Phase 3, week 8)

**What we learn today:**
- Topic words: *en konsert* (a concert), *en utställning* (an exhibition), *ett museum* (a museum), *en teater* (a theatre), *en bio* (a cinema: *gå på bio*, go to the cinema), *ett inträde* (an entry fee: *Inträdet är gratis.*, Entry is free), *en festival* (a festival), *en kurs* (a course), *en hobby* (a hobby), *en förening* (a club, an association), *ett bibliotek* (a library), *ett evenemang* (an event), *fritid* (en; free time), *gratis* (free of charge), *anmäla sig* (sign up).
- **Cards today (load rule 1):** at most 8 new cards in total, error cards included. Card words: *en kurs, en hobby, en förening, ett museum, en teater, ett evenemang, fritid, anmäla sig*. Recognise only, no card: *en konsert, en utställning, en festival, ett bibliotek, ett inträde, gratis, en bio*, which you met on Days 25 and 27. Each error card takes the place of the last card word.
- T6 and T7: *Har du hört om [___]?* (Have you heard about …?) *Det är [på lördag] klockan [___] [på/i ___].* (It's on Saturday at … in …) *Vill du följa med?* (Do you want to come along?) *Skriv till mig före [___]!* (Write to me before …!) / *Vill du komma på [middag] hos mig [på fredag]?* (Would you like to come for dinner at my place on Friday?) *Säg till senast [onsdag] om du kan komma.* (Let me know by Wednesday if you can come.)
- 5 sentences:
  1. *Det finns en gratis konsert i parken på lördag.* (There is a free concert in the park on Saturday.)
  2. *Den börjar klockan sju och slutar ungefär klockan nio.* (It starts at seven and ends at about nine.)
  3. *Inträdet är gratis, men man måste anmäla sig före fredag.* (Entry is free, but you have to sign up before Friday.)
  4. *Kursen är på tisdagar klockan 18–20.* (The course is on Tuesdays from 18 to 20.)
  5. *Tack, gärna! Var ska vi träffas?* (Thanks, I'd love to! Where shall we meet?)
- Grammar: *på tisdag* (this coming Tuesday) versus *på tisdagar* (every Tuesday) (G9); *anmäla sig* needs *sig*; *följa med* needs *med*.

**Why it matters for the exam:** The event e-mail is a writing sample task: what, when and where, two more details, and a proper opening and closing [Official sample]. "Culture" is an official topic area [Official].

**How we learn it (60 min):**
- 0–8 Review: cards from Days 20, 36, 43, 47 and 49.
- 8–13 First study day of week 8: the top-3 error drill (6.1, 3.6) on last week's most frequent error types.
- 13–18 Topic words and sentences.
- 18–33 Speaking: a) an invitation by phone, 4 turns × 20 s [Plan's practice limit]; b) one reaction, 20 s prep + 25 s [Teacher, unofficial]. Record both; redo the weaker one.
- 33–48 Writing (15 min).
- 48–56 Easy-news routine, shortened.
- 56–60 Error log; cards for the card words above (at most 8 new cards in total).

**Speaking output:**
- a) *Ring din vän Sara och bjud in henne till en konsert på fredag. Säg när och var den är och vad biljetterna kostar.* (Call Sara and invite her to a concert on Friday: when, where, ticket price.) The other voice: 1 *Hej, det är Sara!* 2 *Låter kul! När och var är den?* 3 *Hur mycket kostar biljetterna?* 4 *Okej, var ska vi träffas?* Points (5): the invitation; when; where; the price; a meeting place and time.
- b) *Din granne bjuder dig på kaffe i morgon, men du ska jobba. Svara.* (Your neighbour invites you for coffee tomorrow, but you are working. Answer.) Points (3): thanks and a refusal (core reaction 28, with T8's *Kanske en annan gång?*); a short reason; another time.

**Writing output:** *Skriv till en vän om en kurs som du vill gå på, till exempel en kurs i matlagning eller dans. Skriv vad, när och var, och ge två andra detaljer. Fråga om din vän vill följa med.* (Write to a friend about a course you want to take, such as cooking or dance: what, when, where, two more details, and ask if they want to come.) Required points (8): opening; what; when; where; detail 1; detail 2; an invitation question; closing. About 50–80 words [Teacher, unofficial].

Model: *Hej Sara! Jag har hittat en kurs i thailändsk matlagning på medborgarinstitutet i Esbo. Den är på tisdagar klockan 18–20 och börjar i februari. Kursen kostar 60 euro för sex gånger. Vi lagar mat tillsammans och äter allt efteråt! Man måste anmäla sig före fredag. Vill du följa med? Kram, Alex* (*medborgarinstitut*: an adult education centre; ask the teacher which name is used near you.)

**Done when (Green / Amber / Red):** Green = the call gives all 5 points, the reaction fits and gives its 3 points, and the message gives all 8, on your own. Targets (not gates): 20 s a turn; 25 s for the reaction; about 50–80 words.

**Typical errors to watch:**
- *Vill du följa?* → *Vill du följa med?*
- *Man måste anmäla före fredag.* → *Man måste anmäla sig före fredag.*
- *Kursen är på tisdag* when you mean every week → *Kursen är på tisdagar.* (G9)

[Unreviewed Swedish: have a teacher check it]

- `p98-D2`: p. 98-99, Dialog 2, T12: Leaving a bar without your cloakroom ticket; explain to the doorman, describe your jacket.
- `p113-R17`: p. 113, Reagera 17, T13: A street reporter asks what you think of your home town.

### Day 51: me and my background (Phase 3, week 8)

**What we learn today:**
- Topic words: *ett hemland* (a home country), *en familj* (a family), *en barndom* (a childhood), *en utbildning* (an education), *ett yrke* (a profession), *ett språk* (a language), *ett modersmål* (a mother tongue), *en stad* (a city), *ett uppehållstillstånd* (a residence permit), *ett medborgarskap* (citizenship), *en anledning* (a reason), *flytta – flyttade* (move), *växa upp – växte upp* (grow up), *trivas – trivs – trivdes* (feel at home, like it somewhere).
- **Cards today (load rule 1):** at most 8 new cards in total, error cards included. Card words: *en barndom, en utbildning, ett yrke, ett modersmål, ett medborgarskap, en anledning, växa upp – växte upp, trivas – trivs – trivdes*. Recognise only, no card: *ett uppehållstillstånd*, and *ett hemland, en familj, ett språk, en stad, flytta – flyttade*, which you met on earlier days. Each error card takes the place of the last card word.
- T14 turned to yourself, with these 5 sentences:
  1. *Jag växte upp i Pune i Indien.* (I grew up in Pune in India.)
  2. *Jag flyttade till Finland för fem år sedan, eftersom jag fick ett jobb här.* (I moved to Finland five years ago, because I got a job here.)
  3. *Mitt modersmål är marathi, men jag talar också hindi och engelska.* (My mother tongue is Marathi, but I also speak Hindi and English.)
  4. *Jag trivs bra i Finland, eftersom det är lugnt och tryggt här.* (I like living in Finland, because it is calm and safe here.)
  5. *Jag lär mig svenska för att få finskt medborgarskap.* (I am learning Swedish to get Finnish citizenship.)
- Grammar: the past with *för … sedan*, the perfect with *i fem år* (G6, G9): *Jag flyttade hit för fem år sedan. Jag har bott här i fem år.* After *När jag var liten* the verb comes next (G1). *för att* + infinitive gives a purpose (G10).

**Why it matters for the exam:** "Me and my background" is an official topic area [Official], and the Berätta format (1 min prep + 1.5 min) is the first speaking sample task [Official sample].

**How we learn it (60 min):**
- 0–8 Review: cards from Days 21, 37, 44, 48 and 50.
- 8–18 Topic words; say the 5 sentences with your own facts.
- 18–33 Speaking: 1 min prep (6 keywords) + 1.5 min. Record, listen twice, redo once.
- 33–48 Writing (15 min).
- 48–56 Notice routine.
- 56–60 Error log; cards for the card words above (at most 8 new cards in total).

**Speaking output:** *Berätta varför du bor i Finland.* (Tell why you live in Finland.) 1 min prep + 1.5 min [Official sample timing]. Points (6) [Plan's own]: where you come from and grew up; when and why you moved; what was hard at first; what you like now, with a reason; your languages; a plan or a wish.

**Writing output:** *Du börjar på en ny svenskkurs. Läraren ber alla skriva en kort presentation till gruppen. Skriv vem du är, var du kommer ifrån, var du bor, vad du gör, varför du lär dig svenska och vad du gör på fritiden.* (You are starting a new Swedish course and the teacher asks everyone to write a short introduction for the group.) Required points (7): a greeting to the group; name and country; where you live and for how long; work; why you are learning Swedish; free time; a friendly closing.

Model: *Hej allihopa! Jag heter Alex och jag kommer från Indien. Jag har bott i Esbo i fem år. Jag jobbar som ingenjör på ett IT-företag. Jag lär mig svenska för att få finskt medborgarskap, men också eftersom jag vill förstå mina grannar. På fritiden lagar jag mat och spelar badminton. Jag ser fram emot kursen! Hälsningar, Alex*

**Done when (Green / Amber / Red):** Green = the talk gives all 6 points and the introduction all 7, on your own. Targets (not gates): 1.5 minutes (at least 1); at most 2 pauses over 5 seconds; engine errors go on the error list (aim: 2 or fewer).

**Typical errors to watch:**
- *Jag har flyttat till Finland för fem år sedan.* → *Jag flyttade till Finland för fem år sedan.* (G6)
- *Jag bor i Finland sedan fem år.* → *Jag har bott i Finland i fem år.* (G6, G9)
- *När jag var liten jag bodde i Pune.* → *När jag var liten bodde jag i Pune.* (G1)

[Unreviewed Swedish: have a teacher check it]

- `p35-BD`: p. 35, Berätta D, T14: Life story of a friend or relative: birth, childhood, adult life, now

### Day 52: environment (Phase 3, week 8)

**What we learn today:**
- Topic words: *miljön* (en; the environment), *ett klimat* (a climate), *ett utsläpp* (an emission), *återvinning* (en; recycling), *sopor* (rubbish, plural: *soporna*, the rubbish), *sortera* (sort), *en flaska* (a bottle), *en burk* (a can), *en pant* (a deposit), *kollektivtrafiken* (en; public transport), *en elbil* (an electric car), *en cykelväg* (a cycle path), *ett centrum* (a town centre), *naturen* (en; nature), *ren – renare* (clean – cleaner).
- **Cards today (load rule 1):** at most 8 new cards in total, error cards included. Card words: *sopor, sortera, ett utsläpp, återvinning, kollektivtrafiken, ett centrum, ren – renare, en cykelväg*. Recognise only, no card: *ett klimat, en flaska, en burk, en pant, en elbil, naturen*, and *miljön*, which you met in core reason pair 5/24 on Day 30. Each error card takes the place of the last card word.
- Reasons that fit this topic: 4 *Det är bra för hälsan.* 5 *Det är bra för miljön.* 22 *Det tar mycket tid.* 24 *Det är dåligt för miljön.* 31 *Det blir mycket skräp.* 38 *Det blir mycket trafik.* (31 and 38 are from the reference bank: look them up, no card.)
- 5 sentences:
  1. *Det är bra för miljön att sortera soporna.* (Sorting the rubbish is good for the environment.)
  2. *Man får pengar tillbaka för flaskor och burkar i butiken.* (You get money back for bottles and cans at the shop.)
  3. *Om det blir färre bilar i centrum, blir luften renare.* (If there are fewer cars in the centre, the air gets cleaner.)
  4. *En nackdel är att det tar tid att sortera allt.* (A disadvantage is that sorting everything takes time.)
  5. *Alla har inte tid, men alla kan göra lite.* (Not everyone has time, but everyone can do a little.)
- Grammar: after an *om*-clause the verb comes next (G1); *ett centrum* gives *bilfritt* (car-free) (G7); comparatives end in *-are*: *renare, tryggare*, not *mer ren*.

**Why it matters for the exam:** "Environment" is an official topic area [Official], and the opinion letter with at least three reasons is a writing sample task [Official sample].

**How we learn it (60 min):**
- 0–8 Review: cards from Days 22, 38, 45, 49 and 51.
- 8–18 Topic words and sentences. Give each of the 6 reasons a *Till exempel* sentence on this topic.
- 18–33 Speaking: 1 min prep + 1.5 min [Teacher, unofficial]. Record, listen twice, redo once.
- 33–53 Writing (20 min; the mock limit for this task is 25 [Plan's own]).
- 53–57 Notice routine with a short article, shortened.
- 57–60 Error log; cards for the card words above (at most 8 new cards in total).

**Speaking output:** *Påstående: Alla borde sortera soporna hemma. Håller du med? Motivera din åsikt.* (Everyone should sort their rubbish at home. Do you agree? Give reasons.) 1 min prep + 1.5 min. Points (6): opinion; reason 1; reason 2; one example; the other view; a matching conclusion.

**Writing output:** *Skriv en insändare: Stadens centrum borde vara bilfritt. Skriv din åsikt och ge minst tre skäl.* (Letter to the editor: the city centre should be car-free. Give at least three reasons.) 20 min, about 100–150 words [Teacher, unofficial]. Required points (8): topic line; opinion; reason 1; reason 2; reason 3; one example; the other view; a matching conclusion; then name and town.

Model: *Jag vill skriva om bilar i centrum. Jag tycker att stadens centrum borde vara bilfritt. För det första blir luften renare. Bilar ger mycket utsläpp, och det är dåligt för hälsan. För det andra blir det tryggare för barn och äldre. Till exempel kan barnen cykla till skolan utan att vara rädda. För det tredje blir det mer plats för parker, kaféer och cykelvägar. Å andra sidan kan det vara svårt för människor som inte kan gå långt. Då måste staden ha bra bussar. Men för mig är fördelarna större än nackdelarna. Därför tycker jag att centrum ska vara bilfritt. Alex Kumar, Esbo*

**Done when (Green / Amber / Red):** Green = the talk gives all 6 points and the letter all 8, on your own. Targets (not gates): 1.5 minutes for the talk; about 100–150 words; engine errors go on the error list (aim: 2 or fewer).

**Typical errors to watch:**
- *Luften blir mer ren.* → *Luften blir renare.*
- *För det tredje det blir mer plats.* → *För det tredje blir det mer plats.* (G1)
- *Centrum borde vara bilfri.* → *Centrum borde vara bilfritt.* (G7)

[Unreviewed Swedish: have a teacher check it]

- `p63-OA`: p. 63, Din åsikt A, T13: Bike versus car: which you prefer, pros and cons
- `p63-OC`: p. 63, Din åsikt C, T13: Public transport should cost nothing

### Day 53: society (Phase 3, week 8)

**What we learn today:**
- Topic words: *ett samhälle* (a society), *en kommun* (a municipality), *en myndighet* (an authority), *en skatt* (a tax), *ett val* (an election), *rösta* (vote), *en blankett* (a form), *ett beslut* (a decision), *en e-tjänst* (an online service), *en lag* (a law), *en skola* (a school), *en elev* (a pupil), *en medborgare* (a citizen), *en nyhet* (a news item), *ansöka om* (apply for). The plan lists *FPA* (the social insurance office) as an unverified Finland-Swedish term; ask the teacher.
- **Cards today (load rule 1):** at most 8 new cards in total, error cards included. Card words: *ett samhälle, en kommun, en myndighet, en blankett, en e-tjänst, ansöka om, en elev, en skatt*. Recognise only, no card: *ett val, rösta, ett beslut, en lag, en skola, en medborgare, en nyhet*. Each error card takes the place of the last card word.
- Reasons that fit: 1 *Det sparar tid.* 7 *Det gör vardagen enklare.* 17 *Man kan göra det när som helst.* 23 *Det kan vara svårt för äldre människor.* 34 *Barnen sitter för mycket framför skärmen.* 39 *Man blir beroende av tekniken.* 40 *Servicen blir sämre.* (39 is from the reference bank: look it up, no card.)
- 5 sentences:
  1. *I dag kan man sköta många ärenden på nätet.* (Today you can deal with many matters online.)
  2. *Man kan ansöka om saker hemma, när det passar.* (You can apply for things from home, when it suits you.)
  3. *Det kan vara svårt för äldre människor som inte har en dator.* (It can be hard for older people who don't have a computer.)
  4. *Eleverna borde lämna telefonerna i en låda under lektionerna.* (Pupils should leave their phones in a box during lessons.)
  5. *Det är viktigt att alla kan få hjälp på ett kontor också.* (It is important that everyone can also get help at an office.)
- Grammar: after *som*, *inte* comes before the verb (sentence 3, G2). In an *att*-clause too: *Jag tycker att eleverna inte ska använda telefonen på lektionerna.* (I think pupils shouldn't use their phones in lessons; G2, G5.)

**Why it matters for the exam:** "Society" is an official topic area [Official]. In one practice book, opinion is the largest writing function, 5 of 21 tasks [Book count].

**How we learn it (60 min):**
- 0–8 Review: cards from Days 23, 39, 46, 50 and 52.
- 8–18 Topic words and sentences.
- 18–33 Speaking: 1 min prep + 1.5 min. Record, listen twice, redo once.
- 33–53 Writing (20 min).
- 53–57 Easy-news routine, shortened; society news fits today.
- 57–60 Error log; cards for the card words above (at most 8 new cards in total).

**Speaking output:** *Påstående: Det är bättre att sköta ärenden med myndigheter på nätet än på ett kontor. Håller du med? Motivera din åsikt.* (It is better to deal with the authorities online than at an office. Do you agree?) 1 min prep + 1.5 min [Teacher, unofficial]. Points (6): opinion; 2 reasons; one example; the other view; a matching conclusion.

**Writing output:** *Skriv en insändare: Eleverna borde inte få använda mobiltelefoner i skolan. Skriv din åsikt och ge minst tre skäl.* (Letter to the editor: pupils should not be allowed to use mobile phones at school.) 20 min, about 100–150 words. Required points (8), as on Day 52. Give your opinion only; do not state what any law says.

Outline to complete: *Jag vill skriva om mobiltelefoner i skolan. Jag håller med om påståendet. För det första lär sig eleverna mer, eftersom de lyssnar bättre. För det andra sitter barnen redan för mycket framför skärmen. För det tredje pratar eleverna mer med varandra på rasterna. Å andra sidan vill föräldrar kunna nå barnen. Men telefonen kan ligga i väskan. Därför tycker jag att …* (add the conclusion, one example and your name)

**Done when (Green / Amber / Red):** Green = the talk gives all 6 points and the letter all 8, on your own. Targets (not gates): 1.5 minutes for the talk; about 100–150 words; engine errors go on the error list (aim: 2 or fewer).

**Typical errors to watch:**
- *Jag tycker att eleverna ska inte använda telefonen.* → *Jag tycker att eleverna inte ska använda telefonen.* (G2)
- *För det andra barnen sitter för mycket framför skärmen.* → *För det andra sitter barnen för mycket framför skärmen.* (G1)
- *… för äldre som har inte en dator.* → *… för äldre som inte har en dator.* (G2)

[Unreviewed Swedish: have a teacher check it]

- `p124-OA`: p. 124, Din åsikt A, T13: How equal your current country is; where equality is lacking.

### Day 54: phone mix, both roles (Phase 3, week 8)

**What we learn today:** no new topic list. The four phone templates at speed, in both roles.
- T1: *Hej, det här är [för- och efternamn].* (Hi, this is …) *Jag ringer angående [___].* (I'm calling about …) / *Jag ringer för att [___].* (I'm calling to …) *Okej, då säger vi [torsdag klockan tio].* (OK, so we say Thursday at ten.) *Tack för hjälpen. Hej då!* / *Då hörs vi. Hej då!* (Talk soon. Bye!)
- T3 seller: *Ja, den finns kvar.* (Yes, it's still available.) *Den är [tre] år gammal.* *Den är i [mycket] bra skick.* (It's in very good condition.) *Tyvärr kan jag inte gå ner så mycket i pris, men du kan få den för [130] euro.* (I can't lower the price that much, but you can have it for 130 euro.) T3 buyer: *Jag ringer angående [cykeln] i din annons. Finns den kvar?* *Kan du tänka dig att sälja den för [100] euro?* (Would you consider selling it for 100 euro?)
- T4: *Den är fortfarande trasig.* (It is still broken.) *Problemet är att den inte [startar].* (The problem is that it doesn't start.) *Det är bråttom, eftersom [___].* (It's urgent, because …)
- T2, for the message: *Jag skriver för att fråga om [___].* (I'm writing to ask about …) *Jag undrar om [___].* (I wonder whether …) *Tack på förhand.* (Thanks in advance.)
- Rescue rule (2.8): if you miss the voice's line, say *Okej.* and give the card's next point.
- Ett-words need *det*: *skrivbordet* (the desk) → *Finns det kvar? Det är fem år gammalt.* (G7)

**Why it matters for the exam:** Two of the four speaking sample tasks are phone dialogues with a recorded voice: one where you sell, and one where you complain about a repair [Official sample].

**How we learn it (60 min):**
- 0–8 Review: cards from Days 24, 40, 47, 51 and 53.
- 8–14 Say T1–T4 aloud, both roles.
- 14–36 Three calls back to back, 6 turns × 20 s each [Plan's practice limit]. Record all; listen once for points.
- 36–51 Writing (15 min).
- 51–57 Easy-news routine, shortened.
- 57–60 Error log and cards.

**Speaking output:**
- a) Seller: *Du flyttar och säljer din tvättmaskin. En köpare ringer.* (You are moving and selling your washing machine.) The other voice: 1 *Hej, jag ringer om tvättmaskinen. Finns den kvar?* 2 *Hur gammal är den?* 3 *Fungerar den bra?* 4 *Vad kostar den?* 5 *Kan du ta 120 euro?* 6 *Kan jag hämta den i kväll?* Points (6): still available; age; condition; price; an answer to the offer; a time and your address.
- b) Buyer: *Du har sett en annons om ett begagnat skrivbord. Ring säljaren. Fråga om storlek, skick och pris, försök få ett lägre pris och bestäm en tid.* (Call about a used desk: size, condition, price; try to get it cheaper; agree a time.) The other voice: 1 *Hej, det är Mia.* 2 *Ja, det finns kvar. Vad vill du veta?* 3 *Det är 120 centimeter långt och vitt, och det är i bra skick.* 4 *Det kostar 70 euro.* 5 *Hmm, jag kan ta 60 euro.* 6 *Du kan komma i morgon efter fem. Jag bor på Ängsvägen 3.* Points (6): who you are and which ad; size; condition; price; an offer; a time, confirmed with T1.
- c) Complaint: *Din cykel var på service förra veckan, men bromsarna fungerar fortfarande inte. Ring cykelbutiken.* (Your bike was serviced last week, but the brakes still don't work; *en broms* = a brake.) The other voice: 1 *Cykelbutiken, hej.* 2 *Vad gäller det?* 3 *När var cykeln här?* 4 *Vad är det för fel?* 5 *Vi har mycket att göra. Vi kan titta på den om två veckor.* 6 *Okej, kom in med cykeln på torsdag.* Points (7): who; what it concerns; when; the fault; a protest with a reason (reason 25, *Det kan vara farligt.*); what you want and by when; a close saying you will not pay again.

**Writing output:** *Skriv till ett hotell i Åbo. Du vill boka ett dubbelrum för två nätter i juli. Fråga vad det kostar, om frukost ingår och om det finns parkering. Ge dina kontaktuppgifter.* (Write to a hotel in Turku to book a double room for two nights in July. Ask the price, whether breakfast is included and whether there is parking.) Required points (7): formal opening; who you are; what you want (room, nights, dates); question 1; question 2; question 3; contact details with a formal closing.

**Done when (Green / Amber / Red):** Green = each call gives all its points and the message all 7, on your own. Targets (not gates): every turn inside 20 s; at least one indirect question in normal order (*Jag undrar om frukost ingår.*).

**Typical errors to watch:**
- *Finns den kvar?* about *skrivbordet* → *Finns det kvar?* (G7)
- *Okej, då vi säger i morgon.* → *Okej, då säger vi i morgon.* (G1)
- *Jag undrar om ingår frukost.* → *Jag undrar om frukost ingår.* (G3)

[Unreviewed Swedish: have a teacher check it]

**Book practice (*Förbered dig för allmän språkexamen*, pointers only; descriptions are our own words):** 2 task(s) today. Each replaces one of today's own prompts, or the redo of one; if nothing fits, it waits for a 90-minute day (see the note at the top of this file).
- `p30-D10`: p. 30-31, Dialog 10 (phone), T2: Return a courier's missed-delivery call: identify yourself, agree a new delivery time
- `p51-R14`: p. 51, Reagera 14 (phone), T3: Phone a seller about a used washing machine ad for more details

### Day 55: Mock 2 preparation (Phase 3, week 8)

**What we learn today:** no new topic list and no new chunks. The pre-mock warm-up (3.3): five random prompts, each from a different engine (seen practice), first two sentences only; then the 10 core reactions and the 12 core reason pairs from the English cue. Then one short task of each type.

**Why it matters for the exam:** Mock 2 tomorrow is a full set. When the templates are warm, the first sentence of every task comes without thinking, and a fixed first sentence is the plan's first rescue rule against freezing (2.8).

**How we learn it (60 min):**
- 0–8 Review: cards from Days 25, 41, 48, 52 and 54.
- 8–18 Five random prompts, each from a different engine: read the card, say the first two sentences, next card.
- 18–24 The 10 core reactions and the 12 core reason pairs from the English cue, fast.
- 24–40 One task of each speaking type, timed and recorded. Tick the points only; no redo.
- 40–54 One formal writing task (14 min).
- 54–58 Notice routine, shortened.
- 58–60 Check with the helper: tomorrow's prompts are ready, with a timer, a recorder and a quiet room for about 2.5 hours.

**Speaking output:**
- Berätta, 1 min prep + 1.5 min: *Berätta om en plats som du tycker om.* (Tell about a place you like.)
- Call, 6 turns × 20 s (the helper improvises, or you write 6 questions first): *Ring simhallen och fråga om simkurser för barn.* (Call the swimming hall and ask about swimming courses for children.)
- Voicemail, 20 s: *Du kan inte komma på fotbollsträningen i kväll. Lämna ett meddelande till tränaren.* (You can't come to football practice tonight. Leave the coach a message.)
- Three reactions, 20 s prep + 25 s each: *Din vän har fått tvillingar.* (Your friend has had twins.) / *Din kollegas plånbok är borta.* (Your colleague's wallet is missing.) / *En granne tackar dig för att du hjälpte honom.* (A neighbour thanks you for helping him.)
- Opinion, 1 min prep + 1.5 min: *Påstående: Det är bättre att ha många korta semestrar än en lång.* (Many short holidays are better than one long one.)

**Writing output:** *Du glömde en blå väska i omklädningsrummet på simhallen i går. Skriv till simhallen. Beskriv väskan, fråga om de har hittat den och ge dina kontaktuppgifter.* (You left a blue bag in the changing room at the swimming hall yesterday. Describe it, ask if they found it, give your contact details. T12, formal.) Required points (6): formal opening; what, when and where; a description (2 details); the question; contact details; formal closing.

**Done when (Green / Amber / Red):** Green = each of the five prompts gets two fitting first sentences, and all 5 speaking tasks and the formal task give all their points, on your own. Targets (not gates): about 10 minutes for the five prompts; the 10 core reactions and the 12 core reason pairs from the English cue (a miss is a card to review, not a fail); every task in time. No new cards except error cards.

**Typical errors to watch:**
- Learning new material the day before a mock. Today is recall only.
- A voicemail over 20 s. About 30 words is this plan's guide for 20 seconds (Day 84).
- Answering thanks with another *Tack!* → *Ingen orsak!* (reaction 18)

[Unreviewed Swedish: have a teacher check it]

**Book practice (*Förbered dig för allmän språkexamen*, pointers only; descriptions are our own words):** 2 task(s) today. Each replaces one of today's own prompts, or the redo of one; if nothing fits, it waits for a 90-minute day (see the note at the top of this file).
- `p37-OH`: p. 37, Din åsikt H, T13: Opinion: comfort and function versus fashion in clothes
- `p121-D2`: p. 121-122, Dialog 2 (phone), T12: Tell the insurer about damage at home; explain a missed bill, arrange a visit.

### Day 56: Mock 2, a full mock day (Phase 3, week 8)

**What we learn today:** nothing new: this is the full four-weekly mock (section 6.2), with no new cards except the mock's error cards. Mock 2 is a progress check, so printed prompts may be used (plan 6.2): the blocks below are seen practice, and the helper changes their details. Fresh prompts from the teacher are better if they exist.

**Why it matters for the exam:** It runs the speaking and writing formats back to back under time, which builds stamina for a test day of about 5 hours including breaks [Official]. Mock 2 is a progress check and gates nothing: the Phase 3 exit test (Days 62–63) gates the phase, and only Mock 3 and later mocks count towards booking (6.4).

**How we learn it (2 to 2.5 hours; split into two sittings if needed, speaking first):**
- 0–5 Set up: timer, recorder, prompts face down. Paper only for the preparation minutes.
- 5–35 Speaking set S1–S6, back to back, recorded.
- 35–40 Break.
- 40–100 Writing set: W1 15 min, W2 25 min, W3 20 min [Plan's own]. Typed, no spelling help, no dictionary.
- 100–105 Break.
- 105–125 Listening and reading: 2 unseen easy-news clips (3 facts and every number for each) and 2 unseen notices (5 questions each: what, when, where, for whom, cost or condition), scored against the text.
- 125–145 Count (6.3). The teacher rates every task first; then you count points per task, time, word counts, engine errors and the listening and reading percentage. Where the counts differ, the teacher's count stands. Make the mock's error cards and send the teacher pack.

**Speaking output (seen practice):**
- S1, Berätta, 1 min prep + 1.5 min: *Berätta om en vanlig dag i ditt liv.* (Tell about an ordinary day in your life.)
- S2, buyer call, 6 turns × 20 s. Section 6.2 alternates the roles and Mock 3 is a seller call, so Mock 2 is a buyer call. Card: *Du har sett en annons om en begagnad mikrovågsugn. Ring säljaren. Fråga om ålder, skick och pris, försök få ett lägre pris och bestäm när du kan hämta den.* (Call about a used microwave: age, condition, price; try to get it cheaper; agree when to collect it.) The other voice: 1 *Hej, det är Leena.* 2 *Ja, den finns kvar.* 3 *Den är fyra år gammal och fungerar bra.* 4 *Jag vill ha 40 euro.* 5 *Hmm, jag kan ta 35 euro, men inte mindre.* 6 *Okej. När vill du hämta den?*
- S3, complaint call, 6 turns × 20 s. Card: *Du har beställt en ny säng från en möbelaffär. Den skulle komma i går, men ingen kom. Ring affären.* (You ordered a new bed. It should have come yesterday, but nobody came. Call the shop.) The other voice: 1 *Möbelaffären, hej.* 2 *Vad gäller det?* 3 *Har du ordernumret?* 4 *Jag ser att leveransen är flyttad till nästa vecka.* 5 *Tyvärr kan vi inte komma tidigare.* 6 *Jag ska se vad jag kan göra. Vi ringer dig i eftermiddag.*
- S4, voicemail, 20 s: *Du har bokat ett bord på en restaurang i kväll, men du kan inte komma. Lämna ett meddelande till restaurangen.* (You booked a table for tonight but can't come.)
- S5, three reactions, 20 s prep + 25 s each: *Din granne berättar att hon har fått ett barn.* (Your neighbour has had a baby.) / *Din vän har förlorat jobbet.* (Your friend has lost their job.) / *En kollega tackar dig för att du hjälpte henne med en presentation.* (A colleague thanks you for helping with a presentation.)
- S6, opinion, 1 min prep + 1.5 min: *Påstående: Barn borde inte ha läxor. Håller du med? Motivera din åsikt.* (Children should not have homework. Do you agree?)

**Writing output (seen practice):**
- W1, informal, 15 min, about 50–80 words [Teacher, unofficial]: *Du har flyttat till en ny lägenhet. Skriv till din vän Sara och bjud henne på middag. Berätta lite om lägenheten, föreslå en dag och en tid, och skriv hur hon hittar dit.* (You have moved. Invite Sara to dinner: a little about the flat, a day and time, how to get there.) Points (6): opening; the flat (1–2 details); the invitation with day and time; how to get there; a reply-by question; closing.
- W2, letter to the editor, 25 min, about 100–150 words: *Skriv en insändare: Alla borde jobba bara fyra dagar i veckan. Skriv din åsikt och ge minst tre skäl.* (Everyone should work only four days a week.) Points (8): topic line; opinion; reason 1; reason 2; reason 3; an example; the other view; a matching conclusion.
- W3, formal complaint, 20 min, about 50–80 words: *Du har fått en faktura från en trädgårdsfirma. Du tycker att den är för hög. Skriv till firman. Skriv vilken faktura det gäller, ge minst två skäl, föreslå ett rimligt belopp och ge dina kontaktuppgifter.* (A garden firm's bill is too high.) Points (7): which bill (number, date, amount); reason 1; reason 2; a suitable amount; what you want; contact details; a formal opening and closing. The learner's amounts must add up, as in the Day 57 model.

**Done when:** every task is attempted (none left blank) and recorded in two places. Mock 2 is a progress check and gates nothing (6.4).
- **Green / Amber / Red per subtest** (the rule above Day 15, applied to each subtest's tasks together), in the table you started on Day 28. It is an early skill signal, not a decision: the strongest pair may start to show, but the pair, the date and the sitting are still chosen only at booking time (4.7).

| Subtest | Tasks | Mock 1 (Day 28) | Mock 2: G / A / R | Top 1–2 mistakes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Speaking | S1–S6 | | | |
| Writing | W1–W3 | | | |
| Listening | 2 clips | | | |
| Reading | 2 notices | | | |

- **Booking-gate checks** [Plan's own, 6.4], recorded for practice only: every required point covered in every task; every task finished in time; a teacher rating of A or B ("understandable with some errors" or better) on every task. Engine errors per text are counted and tracked on the error list, not gated. Listening and reading about 70% or more, counted only on teacher- or helper-written questions in the three formats (multiple choice, true/false, short open answers) and only relevant for a pair with listening or reading. Write the numbers in your progress record next to Mock 1. Day 57, the error clinic, starts from this mock's errors.

**Typical errors to watch:**
- In S2, no offer or no pick-up time. The card asks for both.
- In S3, missing *flyttad* (moved) in the voice's line. It changes what you must protest about.
- W2 with only two reasons, or W3 with no amount or no contact details. These are required points in the official samples [Official sample].

# YKI level 3 exam-first curriculum, part 3: Days 57–84 and After Day 84

[Unreviewed Swedish: have a teacher check it]

This part covers Days 57–84: the last week of Phase 3 (topic transfer, Days 57–63, ending with the Phase 3 exit test on Days 62–63) and all of Phase 4 (timed full tasks and mocks, Days 64–84, ending with Mock 3 on Day 84). The final section, "After Day 84", gives the weekly repeat cycle and the 7-day taper (Phase 5).

IDs follow the main plan: templates T1–T16, grammar G1–G10, reasons 1–40, reactions 1–30, rescue moves 1–8, and appendix prompts A1–L10 (the Swedish cards are in the plan's appendix; this part gives the ID and a short English gloss, and writes out in full only the prompts it adds itself, marked [Plan's own]). "Engine errors" means G1, G2, G3 and G5. Appendix prompts already used in parts 1–2, or too close to their tasks, are not reused here.

A normal day is 60 minutes: review 0–8, new 8–18, speaking 18–33, writing 33–48, listening or reading 48–56, error log 56–60. Full-set and test days run longer, and their length is given honestly. Tags: [Official sample] = the public practice tasks; [Teacher, unofficial] = a class teacher's format; [Book count] = one practice book; [Plan's own] = a practice target set by this plan, not an exam rule. Blocks marked **seen practice** hold prompts that are printed in this file, so they are not unseen: a helper changes their details, and mocks that count towards booking use fresh prompts instead (plan 6.2). A helper plays the recorded voice in calls; with no helper, write 6 short questions for the other side first and cover them.

**Since the second review (16 November 2026)**, every "Done when" in this part is judged by the Green / Amber / Red rule stated above Day 15 (plan section 1): **Green**, all required points on your own, move on; **Amber**, understandable but hesitant or with a recurring mistake, log the top 1–2 mistakes and move on; **Red**, the card misunderstood, no answer formed, or the model needed, repeat the skill tomorrow with new details. Seconds, word counts, error counts and "N of M" are targets, not gates; spelling and capital-letter slips never make a task Amber or Red. Two parts keep their own rules: the Phase-exit rule (Days 62–63) and the booking-gate checks (Day 84 and every mock after it). Full sets timed at the mock limits run longer than 60 minutes (the writing sets on Days 65, 73 and 79 about 80 minutes, speaking set C on Day 78 about 65); this is a declared exception to load rule 6, like the mocks, and a set is never split across two days. A note on words: an error type "marked red" (3.6) means one seen 3 or more times in a week; it is not the Red task rating.

### Day 57: error clinic after Mock 2 (Phase 3, week 9)

Day 57: see section 5.1 of the plan.
It turns Mock 2's errors into work: the 12-sentence engine drill (wrong, then right) plus 2 new correct sentences for each of your own top 3 error types, then a repair-call redo (*Ditt kylskåp är fortfarande trasigt …*, T4, 7 points) and a 15-minute bill complaint to a painting firm (7 points, amounts that add up).
Any error type seen 3 or more times this week is marked red (3.6). This is the format every later fix day copies.
**Done when (Green / Amber / Red):** Green = the call and the complaint each give all 7 points, with amounts that add up, on your own, and the drill sentences are written. The counts in 5.1 are targets, not gates.

### Day 58: weakest function A (Phase 3, week 9)

**What we learn today:**
- Choose today's function from Mock 2. Count, per function, the missing points and the teacher's ratings. The weakest is the one with the most missing points; on a tie, the lower rating; on a second tie, the one that is more frequent in the practice book (request 38 and complaint 33 of 133 tasks [Book count]).
- Say that function's template card aloud twice (T2, T3, T4/T5, T9, T10, T13, T14/T15 or T6/T7). If its points keep going missing, add the capital-letter line to the top of the card (3.6, rule 4).
- Then the helper gives you the matching row of the menu below.

**Menu for Days 58, 59 and 76 (seen practice).** One row per day. Rows not used stay available for Day 76 and After Day 84. If the weak function is a written one, use the row of its spoken twin: W3 complaint → R3, W2 letter → R6, W1 event message → R8.

| Row | Function | 3 speaking prompts | 1 writing prompt |
|---|---|---|---|
| R1 | Request, booking (T2) | A1 book a hairdresser; A3 extend a library loan; A9 ask shop staff for help with a gift | A2 gym: price, opening hours, free trial |
| R2 | Buying and selling (T3) | B7 buyer, used computer; B9 seller, skis, bargain; B10 buyer, flat for rent | B8 buyer's message about a bookshelf |
| R3 | Complaint (T4, T5) | C2 pizza has not come; C4 tradesperson did not come; C9 dry cleaner lost your suit | D9 late delivery although you paid for express |
| R4 | Late, cancel, apology (T9) | F1 late for a job interview (voicemail); F3 cancel the hairdresser (voicemail); [Plan's own] voicemail: *Du har köpt ett bord och ska hämta det hos säljaren klockan fem, men bilen startar inte. Ring och lämna ett meddelande.* | F2 you can't come to the Swedish course; ask about homework |
| R5 | Reactions (T10) | Two rounds of three: E2, E4, E5, then E7, E9, E10 | F10 a card: you can't come to a friend's wedding |
| R6 | Opinion (T13) | K3 not eating meat; K9 everyone should have a pet; [Plan's own] *Påstående: Barn borde hjälpa till hemma varje dag. Håller du med?* | K8 letter: own house or flat |
| R7 | Tell and describe (T14, T15) | L2 a day you never forget; L4 your job or studies; L10 your first winter in Finland | L3 a friend will stay in your flat: describe it |
| R8 | Event, invitation (T6, T7) | H2 invite Sara to dinner; H5 accept a party invitation and ask details; H8 ask a colleague to a lunch walk | H9 invitation to your child's birthday party |

**Why it matters for the exam:** A function that failed in a mock will fail again on test day unless it is drilled on new cards. Missing required points is the error the official sample tasks make easiest to see [Official sample].

**How we learn it (60 min):**
- 0–8 Review: cards from Days 28, 44, 51, 55 and 57.
- 8–15 Choose the function; say its template twice.
- 15–33 The 3 speaking prompts, back to back, recorded. Timings from 2.1 and 6.2: calls 6 turns × 20 s [Plan's practice limit]; voicemail 20 s [Official sample]; Berätta and opinion 1 min prep + 1.5 min; reactions 20 s prep + 25 s [Teacher, unofficial]. Listen once for points; redo the weakest once.
- 33–51 The writing prompt (15 min; 18 min for a letter [Plan's own]), checked in the 3.4 order.
- 51–57 Notice routine (7.4), shortened.
- 57–60 Error log. Mark the 4 prompts as used.

**Speaking output:** the row's 3 prompts, with the section's default points from the appendix plus each prompt's extra points.

**Writing output:** the row's writing prompt; formal texts also need a formal opening, a formal closing and contact details.

**Listening and reading:** notice routine: what, when, where, for whom, cost or condition.

**Done when (Green / Amber / Red):** Green = all 4 tasks give every point, on your own. Targets (not gates): every task inside its limit; the function's top error type gone from the redo (if it is still there, that task is Amber: keep the type on the error list).

**Typical errors to watch:**
- Skipping a card's extra point, such as the price question in A1 or the counter-offer in B9.
- *Jag har väntat sedan en timme.* → *Jag har väntat i en timme.* (G9)
- *Krya på dig!* to news of a death (E2) → *Jag beklagar sorgen.* (reactions 8 and 12 fit different news)

### Day 59: weakest function B (Phase 3, week 9)

**What we learn today:** the same routine as Day 58 for the next weakest function from Mock 2, using its row in the Day 58 menu (seen practice). Add one check: if today's errors take any grammar error type to 3 or more this week, that type is red (3.6). Do a 10-sentence drill on it today and every day until you have 2 clean days.

**Why it matters for the exam:** The top five functions cover about 86% of one practice book's dialogue and reaction tasks [Book count]. Two weak functions fixed before the Phase 3 exit test are worth more than one function polished twice.

**How we learn it (60 min):**
- 0–8 Review: cards from Days 29, 45, 52, 56 and 58.
- 8–12 Template of today's function, aloud twice.
- 12–17 Red-error drill, if you have one (10 sentences); otherwise say the reasons or reactions that fit today's row.
- 17–35 The row's 3 speaking prompts, recorded, listened to once for points, the weakest redone once.
- 35–53 The row's writing prompt (15 min, or 18 for a letter).
- 53–57 Easy-news routine (7.3), shortened.
- 57–60 Error log; mark the 4 prompts as used.

**Speaking output:** the row's 3 prompts at the Day 58 timings.

**Writing output:** the row's writing prompt.

**Listening and reading:** easy-news routine: 3 facts, then every number, date, name and place.

**Done when (Green / Amber / Red):** as Day 58. Target (not a gate): every sentence of the red-error drill, if any, correct.

**Typical errors to watch:**
- The same memorised line in all 3 tasks, such as *Det är bråttom* (It's urgent) when nothing is urgent. Delete what the card does not ask for.
- *Jag undrar om kan jag få en ny tid.* → *Jag undrar om jag kan få en ny tid.* (G3)
- In a seller call, agreeing to the first low offer. T3 gives a counter-offer: *Tyvärr kan jag inte gå ner så mycket, men du kan få den för [130] euro.*

### Day 60: formal writing set (Phase 3, week 9)

**What we learn today:**
- Three formal templates at speed: T2 (*Jag skriver för att fråga om [___]. Jag undrar om [___]. Tack på förhand.*), T5 (*Jag skriver om faktura nummer [___] från den [datum]. Fakturan är för hög av två skäl.*) and T12 (*Jag vill anmäla att [___]. Det hände [___]. Kan ni [___]?*).
- The formal e-mail frame for all three (the opening and closing fit a formal e-mail, not a phone call): *Hej!* (or *Bästa mottagare,*) and a first line that says why you write, *Jag skriver angående [___].* (I am writing about …); T2 and T5 have their own first lines above. Close with *Ni når mig på [telefonnummer] eller [e-post].* *Med vänliga hälsningar, [för- och efternamn]*.
- One phone refusal (T8): *Tack, men jag behöver inget nytt abonnemang.* (Thanks, but I don't need a new subscription.)

**Why it matters for the exam:** One of the three writing sample tasks is formal: a complaint with which bill, at least two reasons, a suitable amount and contact details [Official sample]. Writing three formal texts in a row trains the points-first habit under time.

**How we learn it (60 min):**
- 0–6 Review: cards from Days 30, 46, 53, 57 and 59.
- 6–11 The call (seen practice), 4 turns × 20 s [Plan's practice limit], recorded.
- 11–50 Three formal texts, 13 minutes each [Plan's own: shorter than the 20-minute mock limit, to build speed]. Write each task's points as tick boxes first.
- 50–56 Notice routine with an official letter, shortened.
- 56–60 Error log.

**Speaking output (seen practice):** G3: a salesperson phones and offers you a new mobile subscription; you don't want it. Points (4): acknowledge or thank; a clear no; a reason; a kind close.

**Writing output (seen practice):**
- T2, A8: write to your child's school; ask when the parents' meeting is and whether there is information in English. Points (6): formal opening; who you are (whose parent); question 1; question 2; contact details; formal closing.
- T5, D4: a removal firm damaged your table and charged more hours than agreed. Points (7): which bill (number, date, amount); reason 1, the damage; reason 2, the hours; a suitable amount; what you want; contact details; formal opening and closing.
- T12, J6: a neighbour has left rubbish in the stairwell for several days; write to the landlord. Points (6): who you are and your flat; what; since when; why it is a problem; what you want; contact details with a formal closing.

Model (J6): *Hej! Jag bor i lägenhet B 12 på Solvägen 5. Det har legat sopsäckar i trapphuset sedan i måndags. Det luktar illa, och barnen leker där. Kan ni ta bort soporna och prata med de boende? Ni når mig på 040 123 4567. Med vänliga hälsningar, Alex Kumar* (*en sopsäck* = a rubbish bag; *de boende* = the residents.)

**Listening and reading:** notice routine on a letter from a housing company or the city.

**Done when (Green / Amber / Red):** Green = each text covers all its points and the call gives all 4, on your own. Targets (not gates): 13 minutes a text; engine errors go on the error list (aim: 2 or fewer).

**Typical errors to watch:**
- *Jag undrar när är föräldramötet.* → *Jag undrar när föräldramötet är.* (G3)
- *Soporna har legat där sedan tre dagar.* → *Soporna har legat där i tre dagar.* (G9)
- A bill complaint with no amount. The official sample requires one.

[Unreviewed Swedish: have a teacher check it]

- `p38-S2`: p. 38, Skriva 2, T2: Email an estate agent: home type, location, budget, move date and reason
- `p38-S3`: p. 38, Skriva 3, T5: Complaint to the housing company board about building faults, with deadline and consequence

### Day 61: speaking set (Phase 3, week 9)

**What we learn today:**
- Four first lines, so no task starts with silence: T14 *Jag vill berätta om [___].*; T3 seller *Ja, den finns kvar.*; T4 *Jag ringer angående [___]. Den är fortfarande trasig.*; T9 *Hej [namn], det är [namn]. Förlåt, jag blir [en kvart] sen, eftersom [___].*
- *en barnvagn* (a pram) is an en-word, so *den*: *Den är två år gammal.* (G7)
- Negative duration takes *på*: *Hissen har inte fungerat på en vecka.* (The lift hasn't worked for a week.) This is the wording of appendix C3; teacher to confirm.

**Why it matters for the exam:** The speaking sample has a person talk, a seller call, a repair call and a 20-second voicemail [Official sample]. Running four types back to back is the rehearsal for a full set.

**How we learn it (60 min):**
- 0–6 Review: cards from Days 31, 47, 54, 58 and 60.
- 6–9 The four first lines aloud.
- 9–24 Speaking set, back to back, recorded (seen practice, below).
- 24–32 Listen to each task once for points; listen to the weakest a second time for clarity.
- 32–47 Writing (15 min).
- 47–55 Easy-news routine, shortened.
- 55–60 Error log. Send this week's teacher pack today (2 newest recordings, 2 newest texts); the Day 62–63 exit tasks go in the next pack.

**Speaking output (seen practice):**
- a) L7, Berätta, 1 min prep + 1.5 min [Official sample timing]: a person who has helped you. Use the six-point talk plan (Day 71).
- b) B1, seller, 6 turns × 20 s: you sell a pram. Points (6): still available; age; condition; price; an answer to a lower offer; a time and your address.
- c) C8, complaint, 6 turns × 20 s: the lift in your building has been broken for a week; call the landlord. Points (7): who; which building and flat; the problem; since when; why it is urgent for you; what you want and by when; a close.
- d) Voicemail, 20 s [Plan's own]: *Du ska skjutsa din kollega Jonna till jobbet klockan sju, men du har försovit dig. Lämna ett meddelande.* (You're driving your colleague to work at seven, but you overslept.) Points (4): who; the apology; the reason; a new time or another plan.

Model (d): *Hej Jonna, det är Alex. Förlåt, jag har försovit mig! Jag kan vara hos dig kvart över sju. Om du inte kan vänta, ta bussen, så ses vi på jobbet.* (about 30 words)

**Writing output:** H3: invite your colleagues to a farewell coffee (*en avskedsfika*). 15 min. Points (7): opening; what; when; where; why; a reply-by date; closing. About 50–80 words [Teacher, unofficial].

**Listening and reading:** easy-news routine.

**Done when (Green / Amber / Red):** Green = all 4 speaking tasks and the invitation give all their points, on your own. Targets (not gates): every task inside its limit; the talk 1.5 minutes (at least 1) with at most 2 pauses over 5 seconds.

**Typical errors to watch:**
- *Hissen fungerar inte sedan en vecka.* → *Hissen har inte fungerat på en vecka.* (G6)
- *Det är två år gammal* about the pram → *Den är två år gammal.* (G7)
- *Om du kan inte vänta …* → *Om du inte kan vänta …* (G2)

[Unreviewed Swedish: have a teacher check it]

- `p102-D4`: p. 102-103, Dialog 4 (phone), T4: Call hotel reception about a fault in your room; ask to move, want more.
- `p114-BH`: p. 114, Berätta H, T14: Pets: your own pet, why people keep them, what children learn.

### Day 62: Phase 3 exit test, part 1 (Phase 3, week 9)

**What we learn today:** nothing new. Five of the ten exit prompts (section 4.4; seen practice, so the helper changes their details). The ten together come from eight topic areas and include two less common functions (I3 = T11 advice, J5 = T12 report), as 4.4 requires.

**Why it matters for the exam:** The real prompts are unseen, every instruction is in Swedish [Official], and the topics come from nine fixed areas [Official]. This test shows whether the templates transfer to topics you have not drilled.

**How we learn it (about 60 min):**
- 0–5 Review: a light pass over cards from Days 32, 48, 55, 59 and 61.
- 5–8 Set up: timer, recorder, cards face down, paper for preparation only.
- 8–20 Tasks 1–3, back to back, recorded. About 15 s to read each card [Teacher, unofficial].
- 20–35 Task 5, informal (15 min).
- 35–55 Task 4, formal (20 min). Both typed, spelling help off, no dictionary.
- 55–60 Note the time of each task. Do not mark anything yet.

**Speaking output (seen practice):**
1. C6 (home and housing), call, 6 turns × 20 s: a neighbour plays loud music every night; call the landlord. Extra points: your flat; how it affects you.
2. G10 (work), 20 s prep + 25 s [Teacher, unofficial]: a colleague wants you to give a speech at the party; you don't want to. Points: thanks; a clear no; a reason; an alternative or a kind close.
3. I3 (health and wellbeing, T11), 30 s prep + 1 min [Plan's own]: a colleague sleeps badly; give advice. Points: at least 2 pieces of advice, each with a reason or example; a friendly close.

**Writing output (seen practice):**
4. D3 (shops and services), 20 min [Plan's own]: a jacket bought online; the zip broke after two weeks. Points: when you bought it (order number); what is wrong; what you want; contact details; formal opening and closing.
5. H7 (culture), 15 min: a language café where you can speak Swedish. Points: what; when; where; two more details; an invitation question; opening and closing.

**Listening and reading:** none today (test day).

**Done when:** all 5 tasks are attempted, none left blank, and each finishing time is written down. Each task is rated Green, Amber or Red in the Day 63 count, and the Phase-exit rule is checked there.

**Typical errors to watch:**
- In C6, no flat number. The landlord needs to know who is calling.
- In I3, one piece of advice only. The default points ask for at least 2.
- *Dragkedjan gick sönder efter två veckor, så jag vill har en ny.* → *… så jag vill ha en ny.* (G4)

### Day 63: Phase 3 exit test, part 2 (Phase 3, week 9)

**What we learn today:** the other five exit prompts, then the count of all ten.

**Why it matters for the exam:** Phase 4 is timed full sets and mocks. Starting it with a function or topic that still fails only repeats that failure under time pressure.

**How we learn it (about 75 min):**
- 0–3 Set up as on Day 62.
- 3–15 Tasks 6–8, back to back, recorded.
- 15–40 Task 9, letter (25 min [Plan's own], about 100–150 words [Teacher, unofficial]).
- 40–60 Task 10, formal (20 min).
- 60–65 Rate all ten tasks Green, Amber or Red, and note points per task, time and engine errors per text for the record (6.3). Put all ten in the teacher pack.
- 65–75 All overdue cards.

**Speaking output (seen practice):**
6. L5 (me and my background), 1 min prep + 1.5 min: a holiday or festival (*en högtid*) in your home country.
7. J5 (society, T12), call, 6 turns × 20 s: the street lamp outside your house does not work; call the municipality. Points: who; what; where (address); since when; why it is a problem; what you want; contact details.
8. K5 (travel), 1 min prep + 1.5 min: *Det är bättre att resa med tåg än med flyg.* Points: opinion; 2 reasons; an example; the other view; a matching conclusion.

**Writing output (seen practice):**
9. K6 (society), letter: all adults should learn a new language. Points (8): topic line; opinion; 3 reasons; an example; the other view; a matching conclusion.
10. A10 (shops and services): write to a restaurant; book a table for eight on Saturday and ask about vegetarian food. Points: day; time; number of people; the vegetarian question; contact details; formal opening and closing.

**Listening and reading:** one clip after the count, if time allows.

**Done when:** all ten tasks are rated Green, Amber or Red (rule above Day 15).

**Phase-exit rule** (Days 62 and 63 together, 10 tasks; section 4.4 as revised on 16 November 2026): the phase is passed when **at most 1 task is Red and no Red is a misunderstood question on a required point**. Targets (not gates): every required point covered; every task finished in time; engine errors per written text counted and kept on the error list; a teacher rating of A or B ("understandable with some errors" or better). The teacher's rating can change a mark. With no outside rater the result is provisional (3.5).
- **After a pass:** a Red task (at most one) is repeated with new details in the next day or two, using the matching day of Days 43–61; then Phase 4 starts with Day 64. Amber tasks put their top 1–2 mistakes on the error list.
- **If it is not passed** (2 or more Reds, or a Red that misunderstood a question on a required point): add at most one extra week of topic days for the Red topics or functions (repeat the matching Days 43–61 with new prompts), then retest those tasks before Day 64.

**Typical errors to watch:**
- *På Diwali vi tänder ljus.* → *På Diwali tänder vi ljus.* (G1)
- *Jag undrar om har ni vegetarisk mat.* → *Jag undrar om ni har vegetarisk mat.* (G3)
- In J5, no address. The card's "where" is a required point.

[Unreviewed Swedish: have a teacher check it]

### Day 64: full speaking set A (Phase 4, week 10)

**What we learn today:** no new content. Phase 4 starts once the Phase 3 exit test is passed under the Phase-exit rule (Day 63; 4.5). Today is the first full speaking set: the four official-sample formats plus three reactions, timed, back to back. It is also the first study day of the week, so it starts with the top-3 error drill (6.1).

**Why it matters for the exam:** The speaking sample is a person talk, a seller call, a repair call and a 20-second voicemail [Official sample], with reactions as a further practice format [Teacher, unofficial]. Running them without breaks builds the stamina a long test day needs (about 5 hours with breaks [Official]).

**How we learn it (60 min):**
- 0–6 Review: cards from Days 34, 50, 57, 61 and 63.
- 6–11 Top-3 error drill: last week's 3 most frequent error types, a few new sentences each (3.6).
- 11–14 First lines of T1, T3, T4, T9, T10 and T14 aloud.
- 14–34 Set A (seen practice), recorded, no pauses between tasks.
- 34–50 Listen to each task twice (points, then clarity) and tick its checklist. No writing task today.
- 50–57 Easy-news routine, shortened.
- 57–60 Error log.

**Speaking output (seen practice):**
- S1, Berätta, 1 min prep + 1.5 min [Plan's own prompt]: *Berätta om ditt hem. Var bor du, hur ser det ut och vad tycker du om det?* (Tell about your home.) Points (6): where; what kind of home and rooms; what it looks like; the area; what you like, with a reason; a wish.
- S2, seller, 6 turns × 20 s: B6, you sell a winter jacket. The voice: 1 *Hej, jag ringer om vinterjackan i annonsen. Finns den kvar?* 2 *Vilken storlek och färg är den?* 3 *Är den varm?* 4 *Vad kostar den?* 5 *Kan du ta 30 euro?* 6 *Var kan vi träffas?* Points (6): still available; size and colour; condition; price; an answer to the offer; a meeting place and time.
- S3, repair call, 6 turns × 20 s [Plan's own card]: *Din dammsugare har varit på reparation, men den slutar fortfarande fungera efter fem minuter. Ring butiken. Förklara problemet, protestera och säg vad du vill.* (Your vacuum cleaner was repaired but still stops after five minutes.) The voice: 1 *Kundtjänst, hej.* 2 *Vad gäller det?* 3 *När var den på reparation?* 4 *Du kan lämna in den igen nästa vecka.* 5 *Tyvärr kan vi inte ge dig en ny.* 6 *Jag ska fråga min chef. Vi ringer dig på fredag.* Points (6): who; what it concerns; the fault; that it was already repaired, and when; a protest with a reason; what you want, with a time limit.
- S4, voicemail, 20 s [Plan's own]: *Du ska spela badminton med din kollega Mikko klockan sju, men du måste jobba över. Lämna ett meddelande och föreslå en ny dag.* Points (4): who; the apology; the reason; a new day.
- S5, three reactions, 20 s prep + 25 s each [Plan's own]: a) *Din vän berättar att hon har vunnit en resa till Rom.* (Your friend has won a trip to Rome.) b) *Din granne berättar att någon har brutit sig in i hennes bil.* (Someone broke into your neighbour's car.) c) *En kollega bjuder in dig till sin födelsedagsfest på lördag. Du vill gärna komma.* (Accept.) Points per reaction (3): a reaction that fits; a detail or question; an offer or a close.

**Writing output:** none (the recordings are reviewed instead).

**Listening and reading:** easy-news routine.

**Done when (Green / Amber / Red):** Green = every task gives all its points, on your own, and the checklists are ticked for all 7 recordings. Targets (not gates): every task inside its limit; S1 at least 1 minute 15 seconds with at most 2 pauses over 5 seconds [Plan's own].

**Typical errors to watch:**
- *Jag tycker om min lägenhet eftersom den är inte dyr.* → *… eftersom den inte är dyr.* (G2)
- *Den slutar fungera fortfarande.* → *Den slutar fortfarande fungera.* (G2: *fortfarande* goes where *inte* goes)
- In S5 c), answering an invitation with *Tack!* only. Accept and ask: *Ja, gärna! Vad ska jag ta med?*
