### Day 65: full writing set A (Phase 4, week 10)

**What we learn today:** no new content. One writing set, the three writing formats at the mock limits: W1 informal 15 min, W2 letter 25 min, W3 formal 20 min [Plan's own limits], typed, spelling help off. This is today's single typed task; there is no speaking task, because Day 64 was the speaking set. The day runs about 80 minutes, a declared set-day exception to load rule 6 (see the Part 3 note); if you have only 60 minutes, move the whole set to a day with 80 minutes instead of splitting it.

**Why it matters for the exam:** The writing sample has an informal event e-mail, a letter to the editor with at least three reasons, and a bill complaint [Official sample]. Writing all three in one hour is the hardest stamina test in the plan.

**How we learn it (about 80 min):**
- 0–4 Review: cards from Days 35, 51, 58, 62 and 64 (short pass).
- 4–19 W1. Points as tick boxes first, then write.
- 19–44 W2.
- 44–64 W3.
- 64–72 Count (6.3): points per task, word counts, engine errors per text.
- 72–78 Notice routine, shortened.
- 78–80 Error log. **Send the teacher pack today: all of set A (Day 64 recordings and today's three texts)**, so that the feedback is back for Day 67.

**Writing output (seen practice):**
- W1 [Plan's own]: *Skriv till din vän om en gratis yogakurs i parken. Skriv vad, när och var, och ge två andra detaljer. Fråga om din vän vill följa med.* Points (8): opening; what; when; where; detail 1; detail 2; invitation question; closing. About 50–80 words [Teacher, unofficial].
- W2, letter: the topic is the book task p53-O4 (Book practice below: haggling, *att pruta*, and what can be negotiated). It replaces K10 (school should start later in the morning), which stays a spare for the cycle after Day 84. Points (8): topic line; opinion; reason 1; reason 2; reason 3; an example; the other view; a matching conclusion. About 100–150 words (a target). Core reason pairs that may fit: 3/21 (cost), 8/36 (choice), 16/26 (stress).
- W3, D2: the electricity company's bill is twice as high as usual. Points (8): which bill (number, date, amount); your normal amount; reason 1; reason 2; a suitable amount; what you want; contact details; formal opening and closing. The amounts must add up.

Model (W3): *Hej! Jag skriver om faktura nummer 55120 från den 4 februari. Fakturan är på 180 euro, men jag brukar betala ungefär 90 euro i månaden. Jag tycker att fakturan är för hög av två skäl. För det första var jag bortrest i två veckor. För det andra har vi inte köpt några nya maskiner. Jag tycker att 90 euro är ett rimligt belopp. Kan ni skicka en ny faktura? Ni når mig på 040 123 4567. Med vänliga hälsningar, Alex Kumar* (about 80 words)

**Listening and reading:** notice routine.

**Done when (Green / Amber / Red):** Green = each text gives all its points (W1 8, W2 8, W3 8), on your own. Targets (not gates): each task inside its limit; W2 about 100–150 words; engine errors go on the error list (aim: 2 or fewer per text).

**Typical errors to watch:**
- *För det första jag var bortrest.* → *För det första var jag bortrest.* (G1)
- W2 with a conclusion that does not match the opening (step 5 must agree with step 1).
- *Jag betalar 90 euro i månad.* → *… i månaden.*

[Unreviewed Swedish: have a teacher check it]

### Day 66: listening and reading set (Phase 4, week 10)

**What we learn today:**
- Numbers at speed: the 2.7 traps (*femton/femtio, arton/åttio, halv tre* = 2:30, *kvart i tio* = 9:45), prices, dates and phone numbers digit by digit.
- The 14 meaning-flip words in 2.6 (*inte, aldrig, ingen, men, utan, i stället, tyvärr, redan, fortfarande, inställd, flyttad, ändrad, stängt, försenad*).
- One check line for calls: repeat a number back. *Den tolfte januari klockan sex, alltså?* (The twelfth of January at six, then?)

**Why it matters for the exam:** The listening and reading samples use multiple choice, true/false and short open answers [Official sample], and numbers are the cheapest points (7.1). The phone tasks also start with understanding a recorded voice.

**How we learn it (60 min):**
- 0–5 Review: cards from Days 36, 52, 59, 63 and 65.
- 5–11 Numbers and flip words: the helper reads 20 number items (write digits) and 6 sentences with a flip word; say what changed.
- 11–17 The call, recorded.
- 17–29 The informal task (12 min [Plan's own]).
- 29–53 Timed set: 2 new easy-news clips (about 7 min each: read the questions, listen, answer, listen again, check) and 2 new notices (5 min each).
- 53–57 Score each item as a percentage.
- 57–60 Error log; enter the scores in the progress record.

**Speaking output:** call, 6 turns × 20 s [Plan's own card]: *Ring medborgarinstitutet. Du vill gå en kurs i finska för nybörjare. Fråga när kursen börjar, vad den kostar och hur man anmäler sig.* The voice: 1 *Medborgarinstitutet, god morgon.* 2 *Ja, vi har en kurs för nybörjare. Vad vill du veta?* 3 *Den börjar tisdagen den 12 januari klockan 18.* 4 *Den kostar 85 euro för tolv gånger.* 5 *Man anmäler sig på nätet före den 5 januari.* 6 *Varsågod. Hej då!* Points (6): who and why; when it starts; the price; how to sign up; one number repeated back; a close. (*medborgarinstitut*: an adult education centre; ask the teacher which name is used near you.)

**Writing output:** I6: a friend will visit Finland in winter; write what to pack. Points (4): opening; at least 3 things to pack, each with a reason; one tip; closing.

**Listening and reading:** the timed set. The helper writes about 8 questions per clip and per notice in the three formats (for example 3 multiple choice, 3 true/false, 2 short open answers [Plan's own split]) without showing you the text. With no helper, an AI can write the questions from the transcript; count those scores as training only, because only teacher- or helper-written questions count for the 70% rule (6.2).

**Done when (Green / Amber / Red):** Green = the call gives all 6 points and the 6 flip sentences are understood, on your own; every listening and reading item is scored, with the percentage in the progress record. Targets (not gates): about 90% of the number items right; about 70% on the timed set (counted only on helper-written questions, 6.2).

**Typical errors to watch:**
- Writing 15 for *femtio*. Say the pair aloud once before every clip.
- Missing *inte* in *Kursen börjar inte i januari*. Check every true/false item for a flip word.
- An open answer as a long sentence. Write 1–4 words you heard (7.5).

### Day 67: teacher feedback day (Phase 4, week 10)

**What we learn today:** the fix routine (3.6) applied to set A. Turn the teacher's sheets and your error log into 3 targeted drills, then redo the weakest speaking task and the weakest writing task of set A with one detail changed. With no feedback back yet, use your own count and mark today's result provisional.

**How to build the 3 drills:**
1. List every error from set A in the log by type (G1–G10, word choice, missing point, over time), adding the teacher's top 3 per task.
2. Take the 3 types with the highest "times seen". A type seen 3 or more times this week is red.
3. Make one 5-minute drill per type: a word-order type → 10 new sentences of that pattern on this week's topics; a missing point → 5 old cards, points as tick boxes, then one sentence per point only; over time → cut each answer to the 30-word voicemail guide or 2 sentences per call turn, then re-time it.

**Worked example (G2, *inte* in a subordinate clause).** Log lines: Day 64 S1 *eftersom den är inte dyr*; Day 65 W3 *att vi har inte köpt nya maskiner*; Day 66 call *om jag kan inte komma*. Seen 3 times: red. Drill, 10 sentences, *inte* before the verb after *eftersom, att, om, som*:
1. *Jag ringer eftersom dammsugaren inte fungerar.*
2. *Jag skriver eftersom jag inte har fått pengarna tillbaka.*
3. *Problemet är att fakturan inte stämmer.* (*stämma* = be correct)
4. *Jag hoppas att det inte tar lång tid.*
5. *Jag vet att ni inte har tid i dag.*
Then 5 more of your own; the teacher checks them in the next pack.

**Why it matters for the exam:** "Basic structures often wrong" is a level-2 sign [Official]. Fixing the three most frequent errors removes more mistakes than any new content.

**How we learn it (60 min):**
- 0–6 Review: cards from Days 37, 53, 60, 64 and 66.
- 6–10 Read the sheets; count; pick the top 3.
- 10–25 The 3 drills, 5 minutes each.
- 25–37 Redo the weakest speaking task of set A with one new detail. Record; compare with Day 64.
- 37–52 Redo the weakest writing task with one new detail (15 min).
- 52–57 Easy-news routine, shortened.
- 57–60 Error log; error cards for at most 3 of the drilled sentences (3.3).

**Speaking output:** the weakest set A task again, for example S3 with a coffee machine instead of a vacuum cleaner.

**Writing output:** the weakest set A text again, for example W3 with a water bill instead of electricity.

**Listening and reading:** easy-news routine.

**Done when (Green / Amber / Red):** Green = both redos give every point, on your own, and the 3 drills are done. Targets (not gates): every drill sentence correct; the drilled error types gone from the redos (if one is still there, that redo is Amber: keep the type on the error list).

**Typical errors to watch:**
- Redoing the best task instead of the weakest. The rating sheet decides.
- Drilling 10 sentences that copy one model. Change the subject and the verb each time.
- *Jag hoppas att det blir inte dyrt.* → *Jag hoppas att det inte blir dyrt.* (G2)

[Unreviewed Swedish: have a teacher check it]

And the adapt rule from Day 48: underline the points, pick the template, fill every slot, delete what is not asked.

**Why it matters for the exam:** A reaction, a phone complaint and a formal message are all sample or practice formats [Official sample; Teacher, unofficial]. Mixing them on cards you have not practised is closer to a test than any drilled day.

- Reactions, 20 s prep + 25 s each [Plan's own]: a) *Din kollega har sprungit ett maraton.* (Your colleague ran a marathon.) b) *Din granne har tappat sina nycklar och kan inte komma in i lägenheten.* (Your neighbour lost her keys and can't get in.) c) *En vän frågar om du vill följa med och simma i morgon bitti, men du jobbar.* (Refuse.) Points per reaction (3).
- C10, call, 6 turns × 20 s: you stayed at a hotel; the room was dirty and the shower did not work. Points: who; your dates; problem 1; problem 2; a protest; ask for part of the money back; a close.

**Listening and reading:** notice routine.

**Done when (Green / Amber / Red):** Green = each reaction fits the news with its 3 points, the call gives all its points and the formal text all 7, on your own. Targets (not gates): no turn over 20 s; engine errors go on the error list (aim: 2 or fewer).

**Typical errors to watch:**
- *Jag vill inte att betala avgiften.* → *Jag vill inte betala avgiften.* (G4)
- In c), a refusal with no reason or other time. Use core reaction 28 with a reason and T8's other time: *Tyvärr kan jag inte, eftersom jag jobbar. Kanske en annan gång?*
- In C10, only one problem. The card has two.

### Day 69: opinion and person (Phase 4, week 10)

**What we learn today:** the two long talks and the letter, at full length. T13 (the five steps in 2.2) and T14 with the six-point talk plan. Reasons that fit today's letter: 5 *Det är bra för miljön.* 31 *Det blir mycket skräp.* against: 6 *Det är praktiskt.* (said as the other view) and 36 *Det passar inte alla.*

**Why it matters for the exam:** The letter to the editor needs at least three reasons [Official sample]; the person talk is 1 min prep + 1.5 min [Official sample]; the opinion talk is a practice format of the same length [Teacher, unofficial].

**How we learn it (about 65 min):**
- 0–6 Review: cards from Days 39, 55, 62, 66 and 68.
- 6–12 The T13 and T14 skeletons aloud; one *Till exempel* sentence for each reason above.
- 12–30 Two talks, recorded; listen twice; redo the weaker once.
- 30–55 The letter (25 min, the mock limit), points first.
- 55–62 Easy-news routine, shortened.
- 62–65 Error log.

**Speaking output (seen practice), both 1 min prep + 1.5 min [Plan's own prompts]:**
- *Berätta om en granne eller en kollega som du tycker om.* (Tell about a neighbour or colleague you like.) The six-point plan.
- *Påstående: Alla borde ha en dag i veckan utan mobiltelefon. Håller du med? Motivera din åsikt.* (Everyone should have one day a week without a mobile phone.) Points (6): opinion; 2 reasons; an example; the other view; a matching conclusion.

**Writing output (seen practice):** [Plan's own] *Skriv en insändare: Affärerna borde sluta sälja plastpåsar. Skriv din åsikt och ge minst tre skäl.* (Shops should stop selling plastic bags.) Points (8): topic line; opinion; 3 reasons; an example; the other view; a matching conclusion; about 100–150 words. (*en plastpåse*; in Finland *en plastkasse* may be more common: ask the teacher.)

**Listening and reading:** easy-news routine.

**Done when (Green / Amber / Red):** Green = each talk and the letter give all their points, on your own. Targets (not gates): each talk at least 1 minute 15 seconds (aim 1.5); the letter about 100–150 words; engine errors go on the error list (aim: 2 or fewer).

**Typical errors to watch:**
- *För det tredje det blir mindre skräp.* → *För det tredje blir det mindre skräp.* (G1)
- *Jag har känt henne sedan fem år.* → *Jag har känt henne i fem år.* (G9)
- Using reason 18 *Man behöver ingen bil* in a talk about phones. Use only reasons the topic supports.

- *I går jag glömde …* → *I går glömde jag …* (G1)

### Day 72: fix day (Phase 4, week 11)

**What we learn today:** the fix routine (Day 67) on set B and this week's log: count the error types, take the top 3, make 3 five-minute drills, then redo 2 speaking tasks and 1 writing task with new details.

**Worked example (missing point).** Log lines: Day 71 b) seller call, no address (the card says *säg var du bor*); Day 71 event message, no "where"; Day 68 C10, only one of the two problems. Type "missing point", seen 3 times: red. Drill (5 minutes):
1. Take 5 old cards (Days 54 a, 61 b, 64 S2, 50 writing, 65 W1).
2. For each, write the points as tick boxes in 30 seconds.
3. Say or type **one sentence per point** and nothing else, about 1 minute per card.
4. Add a capital-letter line to the top of the template card (3.6, rule 4). T3: SÄG VAR DU BOR: *Jag bor på [Solvägen 5] i [Esbo].* T6: SKRIV VAR: *Det är [i parken vid stationen].*

The other two drills follow the Day 67 recipe for their type.

**Why it matters for the exam:** The sample tasks list their required points [Official sample], and a missing point is the one error no grammar can repair. Point-checking is a habit, so it is drilled like grammar.

**How we learn it (60 min):**
- 0–6 Review: cards from Days 42, 58, 65, 69 and 71.
- 6–10 Read the teacher's set B sheets (or count yourself: provisional); pick the top 3.
- 10–25 The 3 drills.
- 25–39 Redo the 2 weakest set B speaking tasks with new details. Record; tick the points.
- 39–52 Redo 1 writing task: the Day 71 event message or the Day 69 letter, whichever scored lower (13 min [Plan's own]).
- 52–57 Notice routine, shortened.
- 57–60 Error log.

**Speaking output:** for example set B c) with a broken phone instead of a computer, and d) with a new reason and time.

**Writing output:** the lower-scoring text, with a new event or a new statement.

**Listening and reading:** notice routine.

**Done when (Green / Amber / Red):** Green = both speaking redos and the writing redo give every point, on your own, and the 3 drills are done. Targets (not gates): every drill sentence correct; no drilled error type in the redos (if one is still there, that redo is Amber: keep the type on the error list).

**Typical errors to watch:**
- *Jag bor i Solvägen 5.* → *Jag bor på Solvägen 5.* (a street address takes *på*, as in T3)
- *Du kan komma i morgon efter sex klockan.* → *… efter klockan sex.*
- Redoing a task with the same details as before. Change at least one; otherwise you are memorising one answer (3.4).

[Unreviewed Swedish: have a teacher check it]

### Day 73: full writing set B (Phase 4, week 11)

**What we learn today:** no new content. Three reactions (today's one speaking task, 4 minutes), then one writing set, set B, at the mock limits: W1 15 min, W2 25 min, W3 20 min [Plan's own limits]. The two book tasks replace one reaction and W2's statement, so they add no time. The day runs about 80 minutes, a declared set-day exception to load rule 6 (see the Part 3 note); do not split the set across two days.

**Why it matters for the exam:** All three writing sample types in one sitting [Official sample]; this second full set shows whether set A's errors have gone.

**How we learn it (about 80 min):**
- 0–4 Review: cards from Days 43, 59, 66, 70 and 72 (short pass).
- 4–8 Three reactions (seen practice), recorded.
- 8–23 W1. 23–48 W2. 48–68 W3. Points as tick boxes before each.
- 68–74 Count: points, words, engine errors per text.
- 74–80 Easy-news routine, shortened; error log.

**Speaking output (seen practice):** reactions, 20 s prep + 25 s each [Plan's own]: a) today, the book task p87-R11 (a colleague leaves on maternity leave; Book practice below). The printed a), *Din chef säger att du har gjort ett mycket bra jobb i år.* (Your manager praises your work.), stays a spare. b) *Din kollega berättar att hon inte fick lägenheten som hon ville ha.* (Your colleague didn't get the flat she wanted.) c) *En granne ber om ursäkt för att hans hund skällde hela natten.* (A neighbour apologises because his dog barked all night.) Points per reaction (3).

**Writing output (seen practice):**
- W1 [Plan's own]: *Din vän vill träffas i helgen. Skriv och föreslå en utflykt till en nationalpark. Skriv när och var ni träffas, hur ni åker dit och vad ni ska ta med.* Points (7): opening; the trip; when; where you meet; how you travel; what to bring; closing with a question.
- W2, letter: the topic is the book task p37-OI (Book practice below: how well Finns dress, compared with other countries). It replaces the printed statement [Plan's own] *Skriv en insändare: Det är bättre att resa i sitt eget land än utomlands. Skriv din åsikt och ge minst tre skäl.*, which stays a spare for the cycle after Day 84. Points (8), as on Day 65.
- W3, D8: the dentist has billed you for treatment you did not get. Points (7): which bill (number, date, amount); reason 1; reason 2; a suitable amount (0 euro, or the part you accept); what you want (a corrected bill); contact details; formal opening and closing.

Model (W1): *Hej Sara! Vill du följa med på en utflykt till en nationalpark på lördag? Vi kan träffas vid järnvägsstationen klockan nio. Vi åker buss dit, och resan tar ungefär en timme. Där kan vi gå fem kilometer och grilla korv. Ta med varma kläder, bra skor och något att dricka. Vad säger du? Skriv till mig före torsdag! Kram, Alex* (about 65 words)

**Listening and reading:** easy-news routine.

**Done when (Green / Amber / Red):** Green = each text gives all its points (W1 7, W2 8, W3 7) and each reaction fits the news, on your own. Targets (not gates): every task inside its limit; W2 about 100–150 words; engine errors go on the error list (aim: 2 or fewer per text).

**Typical errors to watch:**
- *Vi kan träffa vid stationen.* → *Vi kan träffas vid stationen.* (*träffas* = meet each other)
- *… behandlingen som jag har inte fått.* → *… behandlingen som jag inte har fått.* (G2)
- In b), *Grattis!* by reflex. Bad news needs core reaction 6: *Vad tråkigt att höra.*

### Day 74: outside functions (Phase 4, week 11)

**What we learn today:**
- T16 directions, as a chain: *Gå ut från huset och ta till vänster.* (Go out of the building and turn left.) *Gå rakt fram i ungefär hundra meter.* (Go straight on for about 100 metres.) *Ta sedan till höger vid busshållplatsen.* (Then turn right at the bus stop.) *Affären ligger mittemot skolan.* (The shop is opposite the school.) *Det tar fem minuter att gå.* (It takes five minutes to walk.)
- T11 advice: *Jag tycker att du ska [___]. Om jag var du skulle jag [___]. Det är en bra idé att [___].*
- T12 report: *Jag vill anmäla att [en bil har stått framför porten i tre dagar].* (*en port* = the front door of a building.)

**Why it matters for the exam:** In one practice book, 8 of 21 writing tasks fall outside the top five functions [Book count]. These moves stop an unusual card from becoming a blank answer.

**How we learn it (60 min):**
- 0–6 Review: cards from Days 44, 60, 67, 71 and 73.
- 6–15 The three line sets aloud; a ladder (3.2) on the directions chain with your own street.
- 15–30 Two speaking prompts (seen practice), recorded; listen once; redo the weaker.
- 30–45 The writing prompt (15 min).
- 45–53 Notice routine.
- 53–60 Error log; at most 8 new cards (3.3).

**Speaking output (seen practice):**
- L8 (T16), 30 s prep + 1 min [Plan's own]: describe the way from your home to the nearest shop. Points (5): the start; at least 3 direction steps; a landmark; how long it takes; a close.
- J9 (T12), call, 6 turns × 20 s: a car has been parked in front of your building's door for three days; call the landlord. Points: who and where you live; what; since when; why it is a problem; what you want; contact details.

**Writing output (seen practice):** I8 (T11): a friend wants to save money; write three suggestions. Points (5): opening; 3 suggestions, each with a reason or an example; closing.

**Listening and reading:** notice routine.

**Done when (Green / Amber / Red):** Green = the directions give all 5 points, the call all its points and the advice e-mail all 5, on your own. Targets (not gates): 1 minute for the directions; engine errors go on the error list (aim: 2 or fewer).

**Typical errors to watch:**
- *Om jag var du jag skulle laga mat hemma.* → *Om jag var du skulle jag laga mat hemma.* (G1)
- *Bilen står där sedan tre dagar.* → *Bilen har stått där i tre dagar.* (G6, G9)
- Directions with no landmark. One fixed point (*banken, skolan, busshållplatsen*) makes them usable.

### Day 75: listening and reading set (Phase 4, week 11)

**What we learn today:**
- A retest of the instruction words: the 13 in 2.6 (*läs, lyssna, välj, skriv, svara, berätta, förklara, beskriv, jämför, föreslå, motivera, rätt/fel, åtminstone*) plus 20 question and meaning-flip words. Targets, not pass marks: 13 of 13 and 18 of 20, the Phase 1 exit (E1) figures; a missed word becomes a card.
- *minst* and *åtminstone* both mean "at least"; *högst* means "at most" (one card).

**Why it matters for the exam:** Every instruction is in Swedish [Official]; a misread instruction costs points before your Swedish is tested (2.6). This is the second timed listening and reading set, so its score can be compared with Day 66.

**How we learn it (60 min):**
- 0–5 Review: cards from Days 45, 61, 68, 72 and 74.
- 5–12 The word test: the helper shows each word once; write the English.
- 12–18 The call (seen practice), recorded.
- 18–48 Timed set: 2 new clips and 2 new notices, as on Day 66, with about 8 helper-written questions per item in the three formats.
- 48–54 Score; compare with Day 66 in the progress record.
- 54–60 Error log; cards for missed words and numbers, at most 8 new cards in total (load rule 1); the rest wait for tomorrow's log.

**Speaking output (seen practice):** J3 (T12), call, 6 turns × 20 s: someone has stolen your bike; call the police. Points: who; what was stolen; when and where; a description with 2 details; what you want; contact details.

**Writing output:** none today.

**Listening and reading:** the timed set. As on Day 66, AI-written questions are training only; teacher- or helper-written ones count (6.2).

**Done when (Green / Amber / Red):** Green = the call gives all its points, on your own; the word test and the timed set are scored, with the percentage next to Day 66's. Targets (not gates): every instruction word and about 90% of the other words (the E1 figures); about 70% on the timed set (helper-written questions only).

**Typical errors to watch:**
- Reading *minst tre skäl* as "at most three". *minst* = at least.
- *Min cykel är stulen i går.* → *Någon stal min cykel i går.* (G6; a finished time takes the past)
- Changing an answer because one word was unknown. Answer from the whole sentence and move on (7.5).

### Day 76: weakest tasks (Phase 4, week 11)

**What we learn today:** your own weak spots from the error log, not new content.
- List every task of sets A and B and Days 68–75 with its share of points and its rating. Pick the 2 speaking types and 1 writing type with the lowest results.
- The helper gives a matching unused prompt from the Day 58 menu (seen practice). If that row is used up, the teacher or helper writes a new card of the same type (4.6 allows teacher-written prompts).

**Worked example (over time).** Log line: Day 64 S4 voicemail, 31 seconds. Before (about 50 words): *Hej Mikko, det är Alex, och jag ringer för att säga att jag tyvärr inte kan komma och spela i kväll, eftersom min chef har bett mig att stanna kvar och jobba över, för vi har mycket att göra just nu, så …* After (about 25 words): *Hej Mikko, det är Alex. Förlåt, jag kan inte komma i kväll, eftersom jag måste jobba över. Kan vi spela på torsdag i stället? Hej!* The rule: who, apology, one short reason, the new plan. About 30 words fits 20 seconds (Day 84).

**Why it matters for the exam:** A turn cut off by the time limit loses its last point, and the voicemail sample is only 20 seconds [Official sample].

**How we learn it (60 min):**
- 0–6 Review: cards from Days 46, 62, 69, 73 and 75.
- 6–12 Make the list; pick the 3 tasks.
- 12–30 The 2 speaking tasks, recorded; listen once for points and time; redo once.
- 30–48 The writing task (15 min, or 18 for a letter).

- 56–60 Error log.

**Writing output:** the weakest writing type, on a card you have not practised.

**Listening and reading:** easy-news routine.

**Done when (Green / Amber / Red):** Green = all 3 tasks give every point, on your own. Targets (not gates): every task inside its limit; a better points share than the same type in set A or B.

**Typical errors to watch:**
- Cutting a voicemail by dropping who you are. Cut the reason instead.
- Picking the task you like, not the lowest one. The numbers decide (6.1).
- *Förlåt att jag är sen* in a voicemail left before the meeting → *Förlåt, jag blir lite sen.* (reaction 22 is for when you are already late; 23 is for before)

Den är ganska stor.* 4 *Den kostar 90 euro.* 5 *Hmm, jag kan ta 80 euro.* 6 *Du kan provcykla i morgon efter klockan fem.* Points (6): who and which ad; the size question; one more question; an offer; a test-ride time; a T1 confirmation.

Vi skickar rätt vara i dag.* Points (6): who; the order number; what you ordered and what you got; a protest; who pays for the return; a close.

- In S2, no offer. Use T3: *Kan du tänka dig att sälja den för [75] euro?*

**What we learn today:** no new content. One writing set, set C, at the mock limits (W1 15, W2 25, W3 20 min [Plan's own]), typed, spelling help off. It is today's single typed task, with no speaking task (Day 78 was the speaking set) and no book task (held back for mocks). The day runs about 80 minutes, a declared set-day exception to load rule 6 (see the Part 3 note); do not split the set across two days.

**Why it matters for the exam:** The last full writing rehearsal before Mock 3; the three sample types again [Official sample].

**How we learn it (about 80 min):**
- 0–4 Review: cards from Days 49, 65, 72, 76 and 78 (short pass).
- 4–19 W1. 19–44 W2. 44–64 W3.
- 64–72 Count: points, words, engine errors.
- 72–78 Notice routine, shortened.

**Writing output (seen practice):**
- W1, H6: invite your neighbours to a clean-up day in the yard. Points (7): opening (*Hej grannar!*); what; when; where; what people should do or bring; what happens afterwards; closing.
- W2 [Plan's own]: *Skriv en insändare: Det är bättre att läsa böcker på papper än på en skärm. Skriv din åsikt och ge minst tre skäl.* Points (8).
- W3, D10: you rented a car, and the firm wants you to pay for damage that was there before. Points (7): which rental (dates, contract number, amount); reason 1 (for example a photo); reason 2 (for example a witness or the pick-up form); a suitable amount (0 euro for the damage); what you want; contact details; formal opening and closing.

Model (W3): *Bästa mottagare, Jag hyrde en bil av er från den 10 till den 12 mars, avtal nummer 4471. Ni vill att jag betalar 300 euro för en skada på bakdörren. För det första fanns skadan redan när jag hämtade bilen. Jag har ett foto från den 10 mars. För det andra står skadan på papperet som jag fick. Därför är 0 euro ett rimligt belopp. Kan ni ta bort avgiften? Ni når mig på 040 123 4567. Med vänliga hälsningar, Alex Kumar* (about 80 words)

**Listening and reading:** notice routine.

**Done when (Green / Amber / Red):** Green = each text gives all its points (W1 7, W2 8, W3 7), on your own. Targets (not gates): every task inside its limit; W2 about 100–150 words; engine errors go on the error list (aim: 2 or fewer per text).

**Typical errors to watch:**
- *För det första skadan fanns redan.* → *För det första fanns skadan redan.* (G1)
- *Jag tycker att jag ska inte betala.* → *Jag tycker inte att jag ska betala.* (or *… att jag inte ska betala*; G2)
- *Efteråt vi dricker kaffe.* → *Efteråt dricker vi kaffe.* (G1)

[Unreviewed Swedish: have a teacher check it]

**Book practice:** none today. Since 16 November 2026 this day's book tasks are held back for mocks (teacher-only file `teacher-only/YKI-MOCK-RESERVE.md`).

### Day 80: mixed prompts 3 (Phase 4, week 12)

**What we learn today:** no new chunks. Three reactions, two calls and one opinion text on cards you have not practised, as close to test conditions as a normal day allows.

**Why it matters for the exam:** Reactions, calls and the letter cover three of the most frequent functions in the practice book [Book count] and two sample formats [Official sample].

**How we learn it (about 65 min):**
- 0–6 Review: cards from Days 50, 66, 73, 77 and 79.
- 6–22 Three reactions and two calls (seen practice), back to back, recorded.
- 22–30 Listen once for points and time.
- 30–55 The letter (25 min).
- 55–62 Easy-news routine, shortened.
- 62–65 Error log.

**Speaking output (seen practice):**
- Reactions, 20 s prep + 25 s each [Plan's own]: a) *Din granne fyller 70 år i dag.* b) *Din vän har missat sitt flyg till Indien.* c) *En kollega frågar om hon kan låna 20 euro av dig till i morgon. Det går bra.* Points per reaction (3).
- G8, call, 4 turns × 20 s: a neighbour phones and asks if you can look after her dog for a week; you are allergic. Points (4): thanks; a clear no; the reason; an alternative or a kind close.
- Call, 6 turns × 20 s [Plan's own card]: *Ring en fotoaffär och boka en tid för ett passfoto. Du kan bara komma efter jobbet.* Points (7): who; what; two possible times; the price; when the photos are ready; a T1 confirmation; a close.

**Writing output (seen practice):** [Plan's own] *Skriv en insändare: Det borde vara gratis att besöka museer. Skriv din åsikt och ge minst tre skäl.* (Museums should be free.) Points (8); 100–150 words.

**Listening and reading:** easy-news routine.

**Done when (Green / Amber / Red):** Green = each reaction fits the news with its 3 points, both calls give every point and the letter all 8, on your own. Targets (not gates): no turn over 20 s; the letter about 100–150 words; engine errors go on the error list (aim: 2 or fewer).

**Typical errors to watch:**
- a) *Grattis på födelsedag!* → *Grattis på födelsedagen!* (reaction 2)
- G8 refusal with no reason: *Tyvärr kan jag inte, eftersom jag är allergisk mot hundar.*
- *Jag undrar hur mycket kostar det.* → *Jag undrar hur mycket det kostar.* (G3)

[Unreviewed Swedish: have a teacher check it]

**Book practice (*Förbered dig för allmän språkexamen*, pointers only; descriptions are our own words):** 2 task(s) today. Each replaces one of today's own prompts, or the redo of one; if nothing fits, it waits for a 90-minute day (see the note at the top of this file).
- `p37-OJ`: p. 37, Din åsikt J, T13: Opinion: do people worry too much about appearance?
- `p115-OE`: p. 115, Din åsikt E, T13: Should children choose their own activities and hobbies? Limits and risks.

### Day 81: fix day (Phase 4, week 12)

**What we learn today:** the fix routine (Day 67) on set C and Day 80, then redo 2 speaking tasks and 1 writing task from set C with new details. These are the last errors to remove before Mock 3.

**Worked example (G6, past or perfect).** Log lines: Day 78 S3 *Jag har beställt den i måndags*; Day 79 W3 *Jag har hyrt bilen den 10 mars*; Day 80 call *Jag har varit där förra veckan*. Seen 3 times: red. The rule: a finished time (*i måndags, den 10 mars, förra veckan, för två år sedan*) takes the past; no time, or "up to now", takes the perfect. Drill, 10 sentences, 5 of each:
1. *Jag beställde den i måndags.*
2. *Jag hyrde bilen den 10 mars.*
3. *Reparatören kom i fredags.*
4. *Jag har redan betalat.*
5. *Den har inte fungerat sedan i lördags.*
Then 5 more of your own, with this week's topics.

**Why it matters for the exam:** Complaints and reports always tell what happened and when, so a past-or-perfect error appears in almost every formal text.

**How we learn it (60 min):**
- 0–6 Review: cards from Days 51, 67, 74, 78 and 80.
- 6–10 Read the set C sheets (or count yourself: provisional); pick the top 3.
- 10–25 The 3 drills.
- 25–39 Redo the 2 weakest set C speaking tasks with new details.
- 39–52 Redo the weakest set C writing task (13 min).
- 52–57 Notice routine, shortened.
- 57–60 Error log.

**Speaking output:** for example S3 with a wrong-size jacket instead of the wrong item, and S4 with a bus instead of a train.

**Writing output:** the weakest set C text with one new detail.

**Listening and reading:** notice routine.

**Done when (Green / Amber / Red):** Green = all 3 redos give every point, on your own, and the 3 drills are done. Targets (not gates): every drill sentence correct; no drilled error in the redos (if one is still there, that redo is Amber: keep the type on the error list).

**Typical errors to watch:**
- *Jag flyttade hit för två år.* → *Jag flyttade hit för två år sedan.* (G9)
- *Jag bor här sedan 2024.* → *Jag har bott här sedan 2024.* (G6)
- Drilling only the error you like fixing. The count decides.

[Unreviewed Swedish: have a teacher check it]

**Book practice (*Förbered dig för allmän språkexamen*, pointers only; descriptions are our own words):** 1 task(s) today. Each replaces one of today's own prompts, or the redo of one; if nothing fits, it waits for a 90-minute day (see the note at the top of this file).
- `p24-D7`: p. 24-25, Dialog 7, T10: Friend just divorced: sympathise, ask how they feel, offer help, ask about children

### Day 82: Mock 3 preparation, light (Phase 4, week 12)

**What we learn today:** no new content. The pre-mock warm-up (3.3): five random prompts, each from a different engine (seen practice), first two sentences only; then the 10 core reactions and the 12 core reason pairs from the English cue. Then one short task of each speaking type, on old cards with one detail changed, and one clip.

**Helper's preparation for Mock 3 (do it today, and do not show the learner):**
- Prepare fresh prompts for Mock 3 (plan 6.2, fresh-prompt rule): the teacher's; otherwise one held-back book task per mock slot (teacher-only file `teacher-only/YKI-MOCK-RESERVE.md`); only as a last resort, prompts an AI writes on the day. Put them face down. The Mock 3 set printed in plan 5.1 is seen practice and does not count.
- If the target pair includes listening or reading: pick 2 new easy-news clips and 2 new notices and write about 8 questions for each in the three formats (multiple choice, true/false, short open answers). Without these, Mock 3 gives no booking evidence for listening or reading (6.2).
- Ready a timer, a recorder, a text editor with spelling help off, paper for preparation notes, and a quiet room for 2 to 2.5 hours.

**Why it matters for the exam:** A fixed first sentence is the plan's first rescue rule against freezing (2.8); warm templates make every first sentence automatic tomorrow and the day after.

**How we learn it (about 45 min):**
- 0–8 Review: cards from Days 52, 68, 75, 79 and 81.
- 8–18 Five random prompts, each from a different engine: first two sentences only.
- 18–24 The 10 core reactions and the 12 core reason pairs, fast.
- 24–35 One short task of each type: Berätta 45 s; a call of 3 turns; a voicemail; one reaction; an opinion of 45 s.
- 35–45 One clip, easy-news routine.

**Speaking output:** the 5 short tasks above, recorded; tick the points only; no redo.

**Writing output:** none.

**Listening and reading:** one new clip.

**Done when (Green / Amber / Red):** Green = each of the five prompts gets two fitting first sentences, on your own, and the helper confirms the Mock 3 kit is ready. Targets (not gates): about 10 minutes for the five prompts; the 10 core reactions and the 12 core reason pairs from the English cue (a miss is a card to review, not a fail).

**Typical errors to watch:**
- Learning anything new today. Recall only.
- Doing a full set today. Save the stamina for Day 84.

**Book practice:** none today. Since 16 November 2026 this day's book tasks are held back for mocks (teacher-only file `teacher-only/YKI-MOCK-RESERVE.md`).

### Day 83: rest (Phase 4, week 12)

**What we learn today:** nothing. 20 minutes of due cards only (the calendar's rest day). No recording, no writing, no mock prompts.

**Why it matters for the exam:** Mock 3 counts towards booking (6.4); it should measure your Swedish, not your tiredness.

**How we learn it (20 min):**
- 0–20 Due cards, said aloud.
- Then check two things: the helper has the Mock 3 kit ready (Day 82), and you know when tomorrow's 2 to 2.5 hours start.

**Speaking output / Writing output / Listening and reading:** none.

**Done when:** the due cards are done and nothing else was studied.

**Typical errors to watch:**
- A "quick" extra mock or a new word list. Both break the rest day.

### Day 84: Mock 3 (Phase 4, week 12)

Day 84: see section 5.1 of the plan.
A full mock of 2 to 2.5 hours on fresh prompts (plan 6.2: teacher-written first, then held-back book tasks, then AI-written on the day; the Mock 3 set printed in plan 5.1 is seen practice): speaking S1–S6 (about 30 min, seller call in S2), a break, writing W1 15 / W2 25 / W3 20 min typed, a break, then 2 clips and 2 notices (20 min), and the count with the teacher pack.
**Done when:** every task is attempted (none left blank) and recorded in two places.
- **Green / Amber / Red per subtest** (speaking, writing, listening, reading), added as a Mock 3 column to the table from Days 28 and 56. It shows which pair is strongest; the pair, the date and the sitting are still chosen only at booking time (4.7).
- **Booking-gate checks** (6.4, unchanged): every required point covered in every task; every task finished in time; a teacher (outside) rating of A or B, "understandable with some errors" or better, on every task; about 70% or more on teacher- or helper-written listening or reading questions, if that subtest is in your pair. The engine-error count is tracked on the error list, not gated.

Mock 3 is the first mock that counts towards the booking gate, which needs two mocks in a row that meet these checks, at least one week apart (4.7); the next mock is in the first week of "After Day 84".

[Unreviewed Swedish: have a teacher check it]

---

## After Day 84: the repeat cycle and the taper

### When this applies

- **Mock 3 alone cannot meet the booking gate.** The gate needs two full mocks in a row, at least one week apart, on fresh prompts, both passing 6.4 for the subtests you will book (4.7). So at least one more week always follows Day 84.
- **Section 4.6 sets the rule:** if the gate is not met after Day 84, repeat Days 64–84 with new prompts; do one full mock every week instead of every 4 weeks; after each mock, spend 3 fix days on that mock's top errors in the Day 57 format; keep cycling until the gate is met at a registration window.
- Phase 4 has no other exit. Its exit is the booking gate (4.5).

### The cycle week [Plan's own split of the 4.6 rule]

| Cycle day | What you do | Time |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Full mock** (6.2), fresh prompts, recorded and typed. The teacher rates first, then you count (6.3). | 2–2.5 h |
| 2 | **Fix day 1, Day 57 format** (error cards, engine drill, a speaking redo, a writing task, the log). Focus: say this mock's errors aloud, wrong then right; 2 new correct sentences for each top-3 error type; redo the weakest speaking task on a new card; one new text of the weakest written type (15 min). Mark red types (3.6). | 60 min |
| 3 | **Fix day 2, Day 57 format.** Focus: 3 five-minute drills built with the Day 67 recipe; redo the second-weakest speaking task and the weakest writing task with new details. | 60 min |
| 4 | **Fix day 3, Day 57 format.** Focus: the mock's weakest function, as on Day 58: the drill, then 3 speaking prompts and 1 writing prompt you have not practised (question-bank book tasks, or seen practice with the details changed). | 60 min |
| 5 | **Next day of the Phase 4 rotation** (below), with new prompts. | 60–80 min |
| 6 | **The day after it in the rotation.** | 60–80 min |
| 7 | **Light day** (3.1): due cards, one task re-recorded with a new detail, one clip; then the pre-mock warm-up (3.3: five random prompts from different engines, the 10 core reactions, the 12 core reason pairs). The helper prepares next week's mock. | 30–40 min |

- **The rotation** is the Phase 4 content days in order: 66, 69, 68, 74, 75, 80, 64 or 78, 65 or 79, then start again. New prompts for the rotation days come from the book map's question bank, or from seen practice with the details changed. The mock, fix and light days (67, 70–72, 76–77, 81–84) are already inside the cycle.
- If your target pair includes listening or reading, put Day 66 or 75 into every cycle week [Plan's own], because only teacher- or helper-written questions give booking evidence (6.2).
- Cards follow 3.3 as before. The mock day makes no new cards except error cards. The weekly teacher pack is the mock itself.

### Where the prompts come from

- **Every printed prompt is seen practice** (plan 6.2, decided 16 November 2026): the appendix, the plan's day entries and this file. Use them on fix days and rotation days with the details changed, never as a mock that counts.
- **Each weekly mock that counts needs fresh prompts,** in this order:
  1. **The teacher writes them** (or briefs a helper), one task per row of 6.2: S1 a person or an experience; S2 a seller call one week and a buyer call the next, with 6 voice lines; S3 a complaint or repair call with 6 voice lines; S4 a voicemail; S5 three reactions; S6 an opinion; W1 an informal message; W2 a letter with at least three reasons; W3 a bill complaint, a product complaint or a request, in rotation; and the listening and reading items with about 8 questions each in the three formats.
  2. **Otherwise a helper draws from the 40 held-back book tasks** (teacher-only file `teacher-only/YKI-MOCK-RESERVE.md`), one per slot. They hold no voicemail and almost no seller, buyer or repair call, so those slots still need the teacher or step 3. They last about three to four mocks.
  3. **Only as a last resort, an AI writes the prompts on the day,** one per 6.2 slot; the helper reads them out, and the learner does not see them before the task.
- The learner never writes their own mock prompts.
- **Appendix prompts not yet used anywhere in this file** (still seen practice, but the least practised, so use them first on rotation and fix days): the Day 58 menu rows not used on Days 58, 59 or 76, plus A4, F7, F8, G1, G2, G4, G7, G9, I1, I4, I5, I7, I9, I10, J4 and J10. Almost none are seller, buyer or repair calls.
- **Close to tasks already done** (use them last): H10, K4, G5 and B5 (close to Mock 3), and A5, C7, E1, F9, G6, H1, I2, J2, J7, J8, K2, L1 and L6 (close to tasks in parts 1–3).

### After each weekly mock: the booking check (4.7)

1. Count the mock by 6.3; where your count and the teacher's differ, the teacher's stands.
2. Record Green, Amber or Red for each subtest in the mock table (Days 28, 56 and 84). Then compare with every row of 6.4 for the subtests you will book (the booking-gate checks, unchanged): every required point; every task in time; engine errors per text counted and tracked (not a pass rule); a teacher rating of A or B ("understandable with some errors" or better) on every task; about 70% or more on teacher- or helper-written listening or reading questions, if that subtest is in your pair. Write "pass" or "fail" and the date in the progress record.
3. The gate is met when **all** of these are true: two passes in a row, at least one week apart, on fresh prompts; both mocks within the 4 weeks before you pay [Plan's own]; Migri's written confirmation, if you plan to combine two partial-day certificates; every item of the 8.3 checklist ticked.
4. Choose what to book with the pair table in 4.7.
5. At each registration window listed in 4.7 (confirm every date and fee before paying), ask one question: **is the gate met today?** If not, let the window pass and keep cycling. Registration is binding [Official].

- With no outside rater you may not book (3.5).
- If the same function fails in two mocks running, spend one teacher session on it [Plan's own, extending the 4.3 rule for a function that fails twice].

### Phase 5: the 7-day taper (section 4.6)

Use it only in the last 7 days before a sitting you have **booked**, whatever the date. It replaces the cycle for those days: no mock and no fix days [Plan's own reading of 4.6]. Use old cards with one detail changed, or leftover appendix prompts. Nothing new is learned.

| Days before the test | Exactly what you do |
|---|---|
| 7 | One timed task of each type: Berätta about a person (1 min prep + 1.5 min); a seller call (6 turns × 20 s); a late voicemail (20 s); three reactions (20 s + 25 s each); W1, an informal message (15 min). All due cards. No new grammar, no new templates. About 60 min. |
| 6 | Berätta about an experience; a complaint or repair call; a cancel voicemail; three reactions; W3, a bill complaint with which bill, two reasons, an amount and contact details (20 min). All due cards. About 60 min. |
| 5 | Berätta about a place or your home; a buyer call; a late voicemail with a new reason; three reactions; W2, a letter with at least three reasons (25 min). All due cards. About 65 min. |
| 4 | Berätta about a person (a different one); a request or booking call; a cancel voicemail; three reactions; W3, a formal request with contact details (20 min). All due cards. About 60 min. |
| 3 | Berätta about an experience (a different one); a repair call; a voicemail; three reactions; your weakest written type from the last mock (15–25 min). All due cards. About 60 min. |
| 2 | Say the core frames aloud once (plan section 1). Do one short timed task (for example a 20-second voicemail). Pack valid photo ID: a passport, ID card or residence permit card, not a driving licence [Official]. About 25 min. |
| 1 | 20 minutes of light review only. Plan the journey with a time buffer: latecomers cannot sit the test [Official]. Pack food and water. Sleep. |
| Test day | Leave your phone and watch: devices are collected [Official]. Read every instruction twice. Answer every item. Never leave a task blank. |

- Days 7–3 are the 4.6 row "one timed task of each type per day". Spreading the three written types across those days is this part's choice [Plan's own].
- Keep the daily 10-minute listening or reading routine on Days 7–3; section 3.7 says not to skip it, and it is not new content.
- If you are ill on the test morning: a refund is possible only for your own illness, with a medical certificate, if you cancel by 08:00 on the test morning [Official] (4.7).

*End of part 3. Before you drill any Swedish here, a teacher must check it.*
